    #!/usr/bin/env python3
    """Derive the winter demand-response programme reports from a season 
extract.
    
    Usage: python3 derive_report.py DATA_DIR OUT_DIR
    """
    
    from __future__ import annotations
    
    import configparser
    import csv
    import datetime
    import json
    import os
    import sys
    import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
    
    LABELS = (
        "SAFETY_HOLD",
        "COOLDOWN",
        "CONTINUITY_WAIVED",
        "CERTIFICATE_PENDING",
        "CLEAR",
    )
    
    
    def to_ticks(wh, wh_per_tick):
        """Wh of a record to ticks, rounded half up."""
        return (2 * wh + wh_per_tick) // (2 * wh_per_tick)
    
    
    def div_half_up(num, den):
        return (2 * num + den) // (2 * den)
    
    
    def div_half_even(num, den):
        q, r = divmod(num, den)
        twice = 2 * r
        if twice > den or (twice == den and q % 2 == 1):
            q += 1
        return q
    
    
    def read_rows(path, delimiter):
        """Header row plus data rows of a CSV/TSV file."""
        with open(path, newline="", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            reader = csv.DictReader(fh, delimiter=delimiter)
            return [row for row in reader]
    
    
    def read_commented_rows(path):
        """Data rows of a TSV file whose comment lines start with '#'."""
        with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            lines = [ln.rstrip("\n") for ln in fh if not ln.startswith("#")]
        lines = [ln for ln in lines if ln != ""]
        if not lines:
            return [], []
        header = lines[0].split("\t")
        return header, [ln.split("\t") for ln in lines[1:]]
    
    
    def fixed_width_lines(path):
        with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            for ln in fh:
                if ln.startswith("#"):
                    continue
                ln = ln.rstrip("\n").rstrip("\r")
                if ln.strip() == "":
                    continue
                yield ln
    
    
    class Offer:
        __slots__ = (
            "oid",
            "event_id",
            "site_id",
            "bid",
            "rel",
            "rec",
            "tot_rel",
            "tot_rec",
            "seg",
            "credit",
        )
    
        def __init__(self, oid, event_id, site_id, bid):
            self.oid = oid
            self.event_id = event_id
            self.site_id = site_id
            self.bid = bid
            self.rel = None
            self.rec = None
            self.tot_rel = 0
            self.tot_rec = 0
            self.seg = -1
            self.credit = 0
    
    
    class Extract:
        def __init__(self, data_dir):
            self.dir = data_dir
            self.rows = {}
            self._load_program()
            self._load_events()
            self._load_sites()
            self._load_offers()
            self._load_profiles()
            self._load_targets()
            self._load_feeder_limits()
            self._load_site_state()
            self._load_waivers()
            self._load_floors()
            self._load_rate_cards()
            self._load_weights()
            self._load_pools()
            self._load_allowances()
            self._load_manifest()
            self._derive_labels()
            self._derive_rates()
    
        def path(self, name):
            return os.path.join(self.dir, name)
    
        # ---------------- loading ----------------
    
        def _load_program(self):
            cp = configparser.ConfigParser()
            with open(self.path("program.ini"), encoding="utf-8") as fh:
                cp.read_file(fh)
            self.wh_per_tick = cp.getint("units", "wh_per_tick")
            self.millicents_per_cent = cp.getint("units", "millicents_per_cent")
            self.region_of_code = dict(cp["region_codes"])
            self.segment_of_code = dict(cp["segment_codes"])
            adders = dict(cp["segment_rate_adders"]) if cp.has_section(
                "segment_rate_adders"
            ) else {}
            self.claim_cents_per_weight = cp.getint("grants", 
"claim_cents_per_weight")
            self.minimum_grant_cents = cp.getint("grants", 
"minimum_grant_cents")
    
            self.regions = sorted(set(self.region_of_code.values()))
            self.segments = sorted(set(self.segment_of_code.values()))
            self.seg_index = {name: i for i, name in enumerate(self.segments)}
            self.n_seg = len(self.segments)
            self.seg_adder = [int(adders.get(name.lower(), 0)) for name in 
self.segments]
    
        def region_name(self, code):
            return self.region_of_code.get(code.strip().lower())
    
        def _load_events(self):
            with open(self.path("events.json"), encoding="utf-8") as fh:
                events = json.load(fh)
            self.rows["events.json"] = len(events)
            self.events = {}
            for ev in events:
                self.events[ev["event_id"]] = {
                    "event_id": ev["event_id"],
                    "interval_count": int(ev["interval_count"]),
                    "cost_weight": int(ev["objective"]["cost_weight"]),
                    "recovery_weight": int(ev["objective"]["recovery_weight"]),
                    "start": 
datetime.datetime.fromisoformat(ev["dispatch_start"]),
                    "uplift": int(ev["uplift_millicents_per_kwh"]),
                }
            self.event_ids_sorted = sorted(self.events)
            # season order: by start instant, equal instants by descending 
event_id
            self.season_order = [
                eid
                for _, _, eid in sorted(
                    (
                        (self.events[eid]["start"], 0, eid)
                        for eid in self.events
                    ),
                    key=lambda t: (t[0], [-ord(c) for c in t[2]]),
                )
            ]
    
        def _load_sites(self):
            rows = read_rows(self.path("sites.csv"), ",")
            self.rows["sites.csv"] = len(rows)
            self.sites = {}
            self.unknown_region_site_rows = 0
            for r in rows:
                region = self.region_name(r["region_code"])
                if region is None:
                    self.unknown_region_site_rows += 1
                    continue
                seg_name = 
self.segment_of_code.get(r["segment_code"].strip().lower())
                self.sites[(r["event_id"], r["site_id"])] = {
                    "event_id": r["event_id"],
                    "site_id": r["site_id"],
                    "region": region,
                    "segment": seg_name,
                    "seg_idx": self.seg_index.get(seg_name, self.n_seg),
                    "beneficiary_id": r["beneficiary_id"],
                    "critical": r["critical"].strip() == "Y",
                }
    
        def _load_offers(self):
            rows = read_rows(self.path("offers.csv"), ",")
            self.rows["offers.csv"] = len(rows)
            self.offers = {}
            self.orphan_offer_rows = 0
            for r in rows:
                key = (r["event_id"], r["site_id"])
                if key not in self.sites:
                    self.orphan_offer_rows += 1
                    continue
                self.offers[r["offer_id"]] = Offer(
                    r["offer_id"], r["event_id"], r["site_id"], 
int(r["bid_cost_cents"])
                )
            # offers.csv rows that are orphans are skipped; others need an 
interval frame
            for off in self.offers.values():
                ic = self.events[off.event_id]["interval_count"]
                off.rel = [0] * ic
                off.rec = [0] * ic
                off.seg = self.sites[(off.event_id, off.site_id)]["seg_idx"]
    
        def _load_profiles(self):
            self.orphan_profile_rows = 0
            self.out_of_range_profile_rows = 0
            self.duplicate_profile_rows = 0
            wpt = self.wh_per_tick
            seen = set()
            count = 0
            orphan_offer_ids = set()
            with open(self.path("profiles.tsv"), newline="", encoding="utf-8") 
as fh:
                reader = csv.DictReader(fh, delimiter="\t")
                for r in reader:
                    count += 1
                    oid = r["offer_id"]
                    off = self.offers.get(oid)
                    if off is None:
                        # an offer id absent from offers.csv is an orphan 
profile row;
                        # an offer skipped as an orphan offer has no site row 
either
                        if oid in orphan_offer_ids or oid not in 
self._all_offer_ids:
                            self.orphan_profile_rows += 1
                            continue
                        # offer exists in offers.csv but was an orphan offer: 
the row is
                        # not orphan by rule 1, only its offer is skipped
                        ic = self._orphan_offer_intervals.get(oid)
                        iv = int(r["interval"])
                        if ic is None or iv < 1 or iv > ic:
                            self.out_of_range_profile_rows += 1
                            continue
                        key = (oid, iv)
                        if key in seen:
                            self.duplicate_profile_rows += 1
                            continue
                        seen.add(key)
                        continue
                    iv = int(r["interval"])
                    ic = self.events[off.event_id]["interval_count"]
                    if iv < 1 or iv > ic:
                        self.out_of_range_profile_rows += 1
                        continue
                    key = (oid, iv)
                    if key in seen:
                        self.duplicate_profile_rows += 1
                        continue
                    seen.add(key)
                    mult = int(r["register_multiplier"])
                    rel = to_ticks(int(r["relief_wh"]) * mult, wpt)
                    rec = to_ticks(int(r["recovery_wh"]) * mult, wpt)
                    off.rel[iv - 1] = rel
                    off.rec[iv - 1] = rec
            self.rows["profiles.tsv"] = count
            for off in self.offers.values():
                off.tot_rel = sum(off.rel)
                off.tot_rec = sum(off.rec)
    
        def _load_targets(self):
            rows = read_rows(self.path("targets.tsv"), "\t")
            self.rows["targets.tsv"] = len(rows)
            self.targets = {}
            wpt = self.wh_per_tick
            for r in rows:
                region = self.region_name(r["region_code"])
                if region is None:
                    continue
                self.targets[(r["event_id"], region, int(r["interval"]))] = (
                    to_ticks(int(r["target_wh"]), wpt),
                    int(r["shortfall_weight"]),
                )
    
        def _load_feeder_limits(self):
            self.ceilings = {}
            wpt = self.wh_per_tick
            count = 0
            for ln in fixed_width_lines(self.path("feeder_limits.txt")):
                count += 1
                event_id = ln[0:10].strip()
                code = ln[10:14].strip()
                interval = int(ln[14:17])
                relief = int(ln[17:27])
                recovery = int(ln[27:37])
                region = self.region_name(code)
                if region is None:
                    continue
                self.ceilings[(event_id, region, interval)] = (
                    to_ticks(relief, wpt),
                    to_ticks(recovery, wpt),
                )
            self.rows["feeder_limits.txt"] = count
    
        def _load_site_state(self):
            self.state = {}
            count = 0
            with open(self.path("site_state.jsonl"), encoding="utf-8") as fh:
                for ln in fh:
                    if ln.strip() == "":
                        continue
                    count += 1
                    d = json.loads(ln)
                    key = (d["event_id"], d["site_id"])
                    if key not in self.sites:
                        continue
                    self.state[key] = (
                        bool(d["dispatch_authorized"]),
                        bool(d["safety_hold"]),
                        bool(d["cooldown"]),
                    )
            self.rows["site_state.jsonl"] = count
    
        def _load_waivers(self):
            rows = read_rows(self.path("continuity_waivers.csv"), ",")
            self.rows["continuity_waivers.csv"] = len(rows)
            self.waiver_signed_missing = 0
            self.waivers = {}
            for r in rows:
                signed = (r.get("waiver_signed") or "").strip()
                if signed == "":
                    self.waiver_signed_missing += 1
                key = (r["event_id"], r["site_id"])
                if key not in self.sites:
                    continue
                self.waivers[key] = (
                    r["routine_certificate_pending"].strip() == "Y",
                    r["waiver_status"].strip(),
                    signed,
                )
    
        def _load_floors(self):
            with open(self.path("segment_floors.json"), encoding="utf-8") as fh:
                doc = json.load(fh)
            floors = doc["floors"]
            self.rows["segment_floors.json"] = len(floors)
            self.floors = {}
            wpt = self.wh_per_tick
            for f in floors:
                region = self.region_name(f["region_code"])
                seg = 
self.segment_of_code.get(str(f["segment_code"]).strip().lower())
                if region is None or seg is None:
                    continue
                self.floors[(f["event_id"], region, seg)] = (
                    to_ticks(int(f["floor_wh"]), wpt),
                    int(f["priority_weight"]),
                )
    
        def _load_rate_cards(self):
            root = ET.parse(self.path("rate_cards.xml")).getroot()
            cards = root.findall("card")
            self.rows["rate_cards.xml"] = len(cards)
            self.cards = {}
            for c in cards:
                key = (c.get("event"), c.get("site"))
                if key not in self.sites:
                    continue
                base = int(c.find("base").text)
                cap = int(c.find("cap").text)
                ov = c.find("override")
                start = int(ov.text) if ov is not None else base
                self.cards[key] = (start, cap)
    
        def _load_weights(self):
            rows = read_rows(self.path("recipient_weights.tsv"), "\t")
            self.rows["recipient_weights.tsv"] = len(rows)
            self.weights = {}
            for r in rows:
                key = (r["event_id"], r["site_id"])
                if key not in self.sites:
                    continue
                self.weights[key] = int(r["grant_weight"])
    
        def _load_pools(self):
            self.pools = {}
            count = 0
            for ln in fixed_width_lines(self.path("pools.txt")):
                count += 1
                event_id = ln[0:10].strip()
                region = self.region_name(ln[10:14].strip())
                cents = int(ln[14:26])
                if region is None:
                    continue
                self.pools[(event_id, region)] = cents
            self.rows["pools.txt"] = count
    
        def _load_allowances(self):
            header, rows = read_commented_rows(self.path("site_allowances.tsv"))
            self.rows["site_allowances.tsv"] = len(rows)
            idx = {name: i for i, name in enumerate(header)}
            raw = {}
            for r in rows:
                site = r[idx["site_id"]]
                ticks = int(r[idx["allowance_ticks"]])
                draws = r[idx["draws_from"]].strip() if len(r) > 
idx["draws_from"] else ""
                raw[site] = (ticks, draws)
            self.allowance_root = {}
            self.allowance_ticks = {}
            for site in raw:
                cur = site
                seen = {cur}
                while raw[cur][1] and raw[cur][1] in raw:
                    cur = raw[cur][1]
                    if cur in seen:
                        break
                    seen.add(cur)
                self.allowance_root[site] = cur
                self.allowance_ticks[cur] = raw[cur][0]
    
        def _load_manifest(self):
            _, rows = read_commented_rows(self.path("export_manifest.tsv"))
            self.rows["export_manifest.tsv"] = len(rows)
    
        # ---------------- derived per-site-row facts ----------------
    
        def _derive_labels(self):
            self.labels = {}
            for key, site in self.sites.items():
                authorized, hold, cooldown = self.state.get(key, (False, False, 
False))
                pending, status, signed = self.waivers.get(key, (False, "NONE", 
""))
                site["authorized"] = authorized
                waiver_valid = (
                    status == "VALID"
                    and signed == "Y"
                    and site["critical"]
                    and site["segment"] == "public"
                )
                if hold:
                    label = "SAFETY_HOLD"
                elif cooldown:
                    label = "COOLDOWN"
                elif pending and waiver_valid:
                    label = "CONTINUITY_WAIVED"
                elif pending:
                    label = "CERTIFICATE_PENDING"
                else:
                    label = "CLEAR"
                self.labels[key] = label
    
        def _derive_rates(self):
            for key, site in self.sites.items():
                start, cap = self.cards.get(key, (0, 0))
                uplift = self.events[site["event_id"]]["uplift"]
                adder = (
                    self.seg_adder[site["seg_idx"]]
                    if site["seg_idx"] < self.n_seg
                    else 0
                )
                rate = start + adder + uplift
                site["rate_pre_cap"] = rate
                site["cap"] = cap
                site["rate"] = cap if rate > cap else rate
                site["cap_binding"] = rate > cap
    
        # profiles bookkeeping helpers -------------------------------------
        def prepare_profile_support(self):
            pass
    
    
    def best_portfolio(ic, opts, target, weight, rceil, cceil, floor_terms, cw, 
rw):
        """Enumerate feasible portfolios and return (key, selected_ids, 
tie_count)."""
        n = len(opts)
        relief = [0] * ic
        recov = [0] * ic
        segrel = [0] * (max(f[0] for f in floor_terms) + 1) if floor_terms else 
[0]
        chosen = []
        rng = range(ic)
        state = {"key": None, "sel": (), "count": 0}
    
        def evaluate(bid, rec_tot, nsel):
            c1 = 0
            c4 = 0
            for t in rng:
                d = target[t] - relief[t]
                if d > 0:
                    c1 += weight[t] * d
                elif d < 0:
                    c4 -= d
            c2 = 0
            for sidx, fticks, pw in floor_terms:
                d = fticks - segrel[sidx]
                if d > 0:
                    c2 += pw * d
            key = (c1, c2, cw * bid + rw * rec_tot, c4, nsel)
            best = state["key"]
            if best is None or key < best:
                state["key"] = key
                state["sel"] = tuple(sorted(chosen))
                state["count"] = 1
            elif key == best:
                state["count"] += 1
                cand = tuple(sorted(chosen))
                if cand > state["sel"]:
                    state["sel"] = cand
    
        def walk(i, bid, rec_tot, nsel):
            if i == n:
                evaluate(bid, rec_tot, nsel)
                return
            walk(i + 1, bid, rec_tot, nsel)
            for off in opts[i]:
                orel = off.rel
                orec = off.rec
                ok = True
                for t in rng:
                    if relief[t] + orel[t] > rceil[t] or recov[t] + orec[t] > 
cceil[t]:
                        ok = False
                        break
                if not ok:
                    continue
                for t in rng:
                    relief[t] += orel[t]
                    recov[t] += orec[t]
                seg = off.seg
                if seg < len(segrel):
                    segrel[seg] += off.tot_rel
                chosen.append(off.oid)
                walk(i + 1, bid + off.bid, rec_tot + off.tot_rec, nsel + 1)
                chosen.pop()
                if seg < len(segrel):
                    segrel[seg] -= off.tot_rel
                for t in rng:
                    relief[t] -= orel[t]
                    recov[t] -= orec[t]
    
        walk(0, 0, 0, 0)
        return state["key"], state["sel"], state["count"]
    
    
    def largest_remainder(funded, entries):
        """entries: list of (beneficiary_id, weight) with weight > 0.
    
        Returns (shares dict, residual cents, residual tie flag).
        """
        total_w = sum(w for _, w in entries)
        shares = {}
        rems = []
        for bid, w in entries:
            q, r = divmod(funded * w, total_w)
            shares[bid] = q
            rems.append((r, bid))
        left = funded - sum(shares.values())
        rems.sort(key=lambda t: (t[0], t[1]), reverse=True)
        for i in range(left):
            shares[rems[i][1]] += 1
        seen = {}
        tie = False
        for r, _ in rems:
            if r == 0:
                continue
            if r in seen:
                tie = True
                break
            seen[r] = True
        return shares, left, tie
    
    
    def allocate_grants(ex, funded, beneficiary_weights):
        """Apportion the funded pool. Returns (shares, allocated, residual, 
tie)."""
        positive = sorted((b, w) for b, w in beneficiary_weights.items() if w > 
0)
        if not positive:
            return {}, 0, 0, False
        claim_rate = ex.claim_cents_per_weight
        claims = {b: w * claim_rate for b, w in positive}
        if sum(claims.values()) <= funded:
            return claims, sum(claims.values()), 0, False
        shares, residual, tie = largest_remainder(funded, positive)
        minimum = ex.minimum_grant_cents
        keep = [(b, w) for b, w in positive if shares[b] >= minimum]
        if len(keep) == len(positive):
            return shares, funded, residual, tie
        if not keep:
            return {b: 0 for b, _ in positive}, 0, 0, False
        shares2, residual2, tie2 = largest_remainder(funded, keep)
        final = {b: 0 for b, _ in positive}
        final.update(shares2)
        return final, funded, residual2, tie2
    
    
    def run(data_dir, out_dir):
        ex = Extract(data_dir)
        regions = ex.regions
        segments = ex.segments
        n_seg = ex.n_seg
    
        offers_by_event_region = {}
        for off in ex.offers.values():
            site = ex.sites[(off.event_id, off.site_id)]
            offers_by_event_region.setdefault(
                (off.event_id, site["region"]), []
            ).append(off)
    
        sites_by_event_region = {}
        for key, site in ex.sites.items():
            sites_by_event_region.setdefault(
                (site["event_id"], site["region"]), []
            ).append(site)
    
        ineligible_offer_count = 0
        allowance_blocked_offer_count = 0
        empty_candidate_groups = 0
        cap_binding_count = 0
        residual_cents_total = 0
        residual_tie_pool_count = 0
        unallocated_pool_count = 0
        tie_decisions = []
    
        allowance_used = {root: 0 for root in ex.allowance_ticks}
        carried = {region: 0 for region in regions}
        results = {}
    
        denom = 1000 * ex.millicents_per_cent
        wpt = ex.wh_per_tick
    
        for event_id in ex.season_order:
            event = ex.events[event_id]
            ic = event["interval_count"]
            cw = event["cost_weight"]
            rw = event["recovery_weight"]
            event_relief_by_root = {}
    
            for region in regions:
                key_er = (event_id, region)
                site_rows = sites_by_event_region.get(key_er, [])
                candidates_by_site = {}
                candidate_count = 0
                for off in offers_by_event_region.get(key_er, []):
                    site = ex.sites[(off.event_id, off.site_id)]
                    if not site["authorized"]:
                        ineligible_offer_count += 1
                        continue
                    root = ex.allowance_root.get(off.site_id, off.site_id)
                    remaining = ex.allowance_ticks.get(root, 0) - 
allowance_used.get(
                        root, 0
                    )
                    if off.tot_rel > remaining:
                        allowance_blocked_offer_count += 1
                        continue
                    candidates_by_site.setdefault(off.site_id, []).append(off)
                    candidate_count += 1
                if candidate_count == 0:
                    empty_candidate_groups += 1
    
                target = [0] * ic
                weight = [0] * ic
                rceil = [0] * ic
                cceil = [0] * ic
                for t in range(1, ic + 1):
                    tt, sw = ex.targets.get((event_id, region, t), (0, 0))
                    target[t - 1] = tt
                    weight[t - 1] = sw
                    rc, cc = ex.ceilings.get((event_id, region, t), (0, 0))
                    rceil[t - 1] = rc
                    cceil[t - 1] = cc
    
                floor_ticks_by_seg = [0] * n_seg
                floor_terms = []
                for i, seg_name in enumerate(segments):
                    fticks, pw = ex.floors.get((event_id, region, seg_name), (0,
0))
                    floor_ticks_by_seg[i] = fticks
                    if fticks > 0 and pw > 0:
                        floor_terms.append((i, fticks, pw))
    
                opts = [candidates_by_site[s] for s in 
sorted(candidates_by_site)]
                _, selected_ids, tie_count = best_portfolio(
                    ic, opts, target, weight, rceil, cceil, floor_terms, cw, rw
                )
                if tie_count >= 2:
                    tie_decisions.append(
                        {
                            "event_id": event_id,
                            "region": region,
                            "tied_portfolio_count": tie_count,
                            "selected_offer_ids": list(selected_ids),
                        }
                    )
    
                chosen = [ex.offers[oid] for oid in selected_ids]
                relief = [0] * ic
                recov = [0] * ic
                bid_cost = 0
                credit_total = 0
                seg_stats = {
                    i: {
                        "ids": [],
                        "relief": 0,
                        "recovery": 0,
                        "bid": 0,
                        "credit": 0,
                    }
                    for i in range(n_seg)
                }
                for off in chosen:
                    site = ex.sites[(off.event_id, off.site_id)]
                    rate = site["rate"]
                    credit = 0
                    for t in range(ic):
                        relief[t] += off.rel[t]
                        recov[t] += off.rec[t]
                        credited = off.rel[t] - off.rec[t]
                        if credited > 0:
                            credit += div_half_even(credited * wpt * rate, 
denom)
                    off.credit = credit
                    credit_total += credit
                    bid_cost += off.bid
                    if site["cap_binding"]:
                        cap_binding_count += 1
                    seg = site["seg_idx"]
                    if seg < n_seg:
                        st = seg_stats[seg]
                        st["ids"].append(off.oid)
                        st["relief"] += off.tot_rel
                        st["recovery"] += off.tot_rec
                        st["bid"] += off.bid
                        st["credit"] += credit
                    root = ex.allowance_root.get(off.site_id, off.site_id)
                    event_relief_by_root[root] = (
                        event_relief_by_root.get(root, 0) + off.tot_rel
                    )
    
                intervals = []
                shortfall_total = 0
                over_total = 0
                for t in range(ic):
                    short = target[t] - relief[t]
                    over = -short if short < 0 else 0
                    short = short if short > 0 else 0
                    shortfall_total += short
                    over_total += over
                    intervals.append(
                        {
                            "interval": t + 1,
                            "target_ticks": target[t],
                            "relief_ticks": relief[t],
                            "recovery_ticks": recov[t],
                            "shortfall_ticks": short,
                            "overdelivery_ticks": over,
                        }
                    )
    
                pool = ex.pools.get(key_er, 0)
                funded = pool + carried[region] - credit_total
                if funded < 0:
                    funded = 0
    
                beneficiary_weights = {}
                recipients = 0
                for site in site_rows:
                    skey = (site["event_id"], site["site_id"])
                    if skey not in ex.weights:
                        continue
                    recipients += 1
                    w = ex.weights[skey]
                    if ex.labels[skey] == "CONTINUITY_WAIVED":
                        w *= 2
                    beneficiary_weights[site["beneficiary_id"]] = (
                        beneficiary_weights.get(site["beneficiary_id"], 0) + w
                    )
                total_weight = sum(beneficiary_weights.values())
                if recipients == 0 or total_weight == 0:
                    unallocated_pool_count += 1
                    shares, allocated, residual, tie = {}, 0, 0, False
                else:
                    shares, allocated, residual, tie = allocate_grants(
                        ex, funded, beneficiary_weights
                    )
                residual_cents_total += residual
                if tie:
                    residual_tie_pool_count += 1
                unallocated = funded - allocated
                carried[region] = unallocated
    
                results[key_er] = {
                    "event_id": event_id,
                    "region": region,
                    "candidate_offer_count": candidate_count,
                    "selected_offer_ids": list(selected_ids),
                    "intervals": intervals,
                    "relief_total": sum(relief),
                    "recovery_total": sum(recov),
                    "shortfall_total": shortfall_total,
                    "over_total": over_total,
                    "bid_cost_cents": bid_cost,
                    "credit_cents": credit_total,
                    "grant_pool_cents": pool,
                    "grant_funded_cents": funded,
                    "grant_allocated_cents": allocated,
                    "grant_unallocated_cents": unallocated,
                    "grants": [
                        {"beneficiary_id": b, "cents": shares.get(b, 0)}
                        for b in sorted(
                            b for b, w in beneficiary_weights.items() if w > 0
                        )
                    ],
                    "compliance_counts": {
                        label: sum(
                            1
                            for s in site_rows
                            if ex.labels[(s["event_id"], s["site_id"])] == label
                        )
                        for label in LABELS
                    },
                    "seg_stats": seg_stats,
                    "floor_ticks": floor_ticks_by_seg,
                }
    
            for root, used in event_relief_by_root.items():
                allowance_used[root] = allowance_used.get(root, 0) + used
    
        os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)
    
        def write(name, obj):
            with open(os.path.join(out_dir, name), "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
                json.dump(obj, fh, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))
                fh.write("\n")
    
        region_total_keys = (
            "candidate_offer_count",
            "selected_offer_count",
            "relief_ticks",
            "recovery_ticks",
            "shortfall_ticks",
            "overdelivery_ticks",
            "bid_cost_cents",
            "credit_cents",
            "grant_pool_cents",
            "grant_funded_cents",
            "grant_allocated_cents",
            "grant_unallocated_cents",
        )
    
        grand = {k: 0 for k in region_total_keys}
        region_summary = {}
        for region in regions:
            events_out = []
            totals = {k: 0 for k in region_total_keys}
            for event_id in ex.event_ids_sorted:
                res = results[(event_id, region)]
                entry = {
                    "event_id": event_id,
                    "candidate_offer_count": res["candidate_offer_count"],
                    "selected_offer_ids": res["selected_offer_ids"],
                    "intervals": res["intervals"],
                    "bid_cost_cents": res["bid_cost_cents"],
                    "credit_cents": res["credit_cents"],
                    "grant_pool_cents": res["grant_pool_cents"],
                    "grant_funded_cents": res["grant_funded_cents"],
                    "grant_allocated_cents": res["grant_allocated_cents"],
                    "grant_unallocated_cents": res["grant_unallocated_cents"],
                    "grants": res["grants"],
                    "compliance_counts": res["compliance_counts"],
                }
                events_out.append(entry)
                totals["candidate_offer_count"] += res["candidate_offer_count"]
                totals["selected_offer_count"] += len(res["selected_offer_ids"])
                totals["relief_ticks"] += res["relief_total"]
                totals["recovery_ticks"] += res["recovery_total"]
                totals["shortfall_ticks"] += res["shortfall_total"]
                totals["overdelivery_ticks"] += res["over_total"]
                totals["bid_cost_cents"] += res["bid_cost_cents"]
                totals["credit_cents"] += res["credit_cents"]
                totals["grant_pool_cents"] += res["grant_pool_cents"]
                totals["grant_funded_cents"] += res["grant_funded_cents"]
                totals["grant_allocated_cents"] += res["grant_allocated_cents"]
                totals["grant_unallocated_cents"] += 
res["grant_unallocated_cents"]
            doc = {
                "region": region,
                "events": events_out,
                "totals": dict(totals, event_count=len(ex.event_ids_sorted)),
            }
            write("region_%s.json" % region, doc)
            for k in region_total_keys:
                grand[k] += totals[k]
            region_summary[region] = {
                "selected_offer_count": totals["selected_offer_count"],
                "relief_ticks": totals["relief_ticks"],
                "bid_cost_cents": totals["bid_cost_cents"],
                "credit_cents": totals["credit_cents"],
                "grant_allocated_cents": totals["grant_allocated_cents"],
            }
    
        segment_summary = {}
        for i, seg_name in enumerate(segments):
            entries = []
            totals = {
                "selected_offer_count": 0,
                "relief_ticks": 0,
                "recovery_ticks": 0,
                "bid_cost_cents": 0,
                "credit_cents": 0,
                "floor_ticks": 0,
            }
            for event_id in ex.event_ids_sorted:
                for region in regions:
                    res = results[(event_id, region)]
                    st = res["seg_stats"][i]
                    floor_ticks = res["floor_ticks"][i]
                    relief = st["relief"]
                    if floor_ticks == 0:
                        bp = 10000
                    else:
                        bp = div_half_up(10000 * relief, floor_ticks)
                        if bp > 10000:
                            bp = 10000
                    entries.append(
                        {
                            "event_id": event_id,
                            "region": region,
                            "selected_offer_ids": sorted(st["ids"]),
                            "relief_ticks": relief,
                            "recovery_ticks": st["recovery"],
                            "bid_cost_cents": st["bid"],
                            "credit_cents": st["credit"],
                            "floor_ticks": floor_ticks,
                            "floor_attainment_bp": bp,
                        }
                    )
                    totals["selected_offer_count"] += len(st["ids"])
                    totals["relief_ticks"] += relief
                    totals["recovery_ticks"] += st["recovery"]
                    totals["bid_cost_cents"] += st["bid"]
                    totals["credit_cents"] += st["credit"]
                    totals["floor_ticks"] += floor_ticks
            write(
                "segment_%s.json" % seg_name,
                {"segment": seg_name, "event_regions": entries, "totals": 
totals},
            )
            segment_summary[seg_name] = {
                "selected_offer_count": totals["selected_offer_count"],
                "relief_ticks": totals["relief_ticks"],
                "bid_cost_cents": totals["bid_cost_cents"],
                "credit_cents": totals["credit_cents"],
            }
    
        summary = {
            "event_count": len(ex.event_ids_sorted),
            "event_region_count": len(ex.event_ids_sorted) * len(regions),
            "site_count": len(ex.sites),
            "candidate_offer_count": grand["candidate_offer_count"],
            "selected_offer_count": grand["selected_offer_count"],
            "relief_ticks": grand["relief_ticks"],
            "recovery_ticks": grand["recovery_ticks"],
            "shortfall_ticks": grand["shortfall_ticks"],
            "overdelivery_ticks": grand["overdelivery_ticks"],
            "bid_cost_cents": grand["bid_cost_cents"],
            "credit_cents": grand["credit_cents"],
            "grant_pool_cents": grand["grant_pool_cents"],
            "grant_funded_cents": grand["grant_funded_cents"],
            "grant_allocated_cents": grand["grant_allocated_cents"],
            "grant_unallocated_cents": grand["grant_unallocated_cents"],
            "regions": region_summary,
            "segments": segment_summary,
        }
        write("summary.json", summary)
    
        site_labels = [
            {"event_id": e, "site_id": s, "label": ex.labels[(e, s)]}
            for e, s in sorted(ex.sites)
        ]
        policy_audit = {
            "compliance_counts": {
                label: sum(1 for v in ex.labels.values() if v == label)
                for label in LABELS
            },
            "site_labels": site_labels,
            "tie_decisions": sorted(
                tie_decisions, key=lambda d: (d["event_id"], d["region"])
            ),
            "cap_binding_count": cap_binding_count,
            "residual_cents": residual_cents_total,
            "residual_tie_pool_count": residual_tie_pool_count,
        }
        write("policy_audit.json", policy_audit)
    
        zero_floor_count = 0
        for event_id in ex.event_ids_sorted:
            for region in regions:
                for seg_name in segments:
                    if ex.floors.get((event_id, region, seg_name), (0, 0))[0] ==
0:
                        zero_floor_count += 1
    
        data_quality = {
            "rows": ex.rows,
            "orphan_offer_rows": ex.orphan_offer_rows,
            "orphan_profile_rows": ex.orphan_profile_rows,
            "out_of_range_profile_rows": ex.out_of_range_profile_rows,
            "duplicate_profile_rows": ex.duplicate_profile_rows,
            "unknown_region_site_rows": ex.unknown_region_site_rows,
            "waiver_signed_missing": ex.waiver_signed_missing,
            "ineligible_offer_count": ineligible_offer_count,
            "allowance_blocked_offer_count": allowance_blocked_offer_count,
            "empty_candidate_groups": empty_candidate_groups,
            "zero_floor_count": zero_floor_count,
            "unallocated_pool_count": unallocated_pool_count,
        }
        write("data_quality.json", data_quality)
    
    
    def main(argv):
        if len(argv) != 3:
            sys.stderr.write("usage: derive_report.py DATA_DIR OUT_DIR\n")
            return 2
        sys.setrecursionlimit(10000)
        run(argv[1], argv[2])
        return 0
    
    
    if __name__ == "__main__":
        sys.exit(main(sys.argv))
