    #!/usr/bin/env python3
    """Derive the winter demand-response programme reports from a season 
extract.
    
    Usage: python3 derive_report.py DATA_DIR OUT_DIR
    """
    
    from __future__ import annotations
    
    import configparser
    import csv
    import datetime
    import json
    import os
    import sys
    import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
    
    LABELS = (
        "SAFETY_HOLD",
        "COOLDOWN",
        "CONTINUITY_WAIVED",
        "CERTIFICATE_PENDING",
        "CLEAR",
    )
    
    
    def to_ticks(wh, wh_per_tick):
        """A record's Wh value as ticks, rounded half up."""
        return (2 * wh + wh_per_tick) // (2 * wh_per_tick)
    
    
    def div_half_up(num, den):
        return (2 * num + den) // (2 * den)
    
    
    def div_half_even(num, den):
        q, r = divmod(num, den)
        twice = 2 * r
        if twice > den or (twice == den and q % 2 == 1):
            q += 1
        return q
    
    
    def desc_key(text):
        """Sort key that orders strings descending inside an ascending sort."""
        return [-ord(c) for c in text]
    
    
    def read_rows(path, delimiter):
        with open(path, newline="", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            return list(csv.DictReader(fh, delimiter=delimiter))
    
    
    def read_commented_rows(path):
        """Header and data rows of a TSV file whose comment lines start with 
'#'."""
        with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            lines = [ln.rstrip("\n").rstrip("\r") for ln in fh if not 
ln.startswith("#")]
        lines = [ln for ln in lines if ln.strip() != ""]
        if not lines:
            return [], []
        return lines[0].split("\t"), [ln.split("\t") for ln in lines[1:]]
    
    
    def fixed_width_lines(path):
        with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            for ln in fh:
                if ln.startswith("#"):
                    continue
                ln = ln.rstrip("\n").rstrip("\r")
                if ln.strip() == "":
                    continue
                yield ln
    
    
    class Offer:
        __slots__ = ("oid", "event_id", "site_id", "bid", "rel", "rec",
                     "tot_rel", "tot_rec", "seg")
    
        def __init__(self, oid, event_id, site_id, bid, ic):
            self.oid = oid
            self.event_id = event_id
            self.site_id = site_id
            self.bid = bid
            self.rel = [0] * ic
            self.rec = [0] * ic
            self.tot_rel = 0
            self.tot_rec = 0
            self.seg = -1
    
    
    class Extract:
        """One season extract, parsed and reduced to the facts the reports 
need."""
    
        def __init__(self, data_dir):
            self.dir = data_dir
            self.rows = {}
            self._load_program()
            self._load_events()
            self._load_sites()
            self._load_offers()
            self._load_profiles()
            self._load_targets()
            self._load_feeder_limits()
            self._load_site_state()
            self._load_waivers()
            self._load_floors()
            self._load_rate_cards()
            self._load_weights()
            self._load_pools()
            self._load_allowances()
            self._load_manifest()
            self._derive_labels()
            self._derive_rates()
    
        def path(self, name):
            return os.path.join(self.dir, name)
    
        def region_name(self, code):
            return self.region_of_code.get(code.strip().lower())
    
        def segment_name(self, code):
            return self.segment_of_code.get(str(code).strip().lower())
    
        # ---------------- loading ----------------
    
        def _load_program(self):
            cp = configparser.ConfigParser()
            with open(self.path("program.ini"), encoding="utf-8") as fh:
                cp.read_file(fh)
            self.wh_per_tick = cp.getint("units", "wh_per_tick")
            self.millicents_per_cent = cp.getint("units", "millicents_per_cent")
            self.region_of_code = dict(cp["region_codes"])
            self.segment_of_code = dict(cp["segment_codes"])
            adders = (
                dict(cp["segment_rate_adders"])
                if cp.has_section("segment_rate_adders")
                else {}
            )
            self.claim_cents_per_weight = cp.getint("grants", 
"claim_cents_per_weight")
            self.minimum_grant_cents = cp.getint("grants", 
"minimum_grant_cents")
    
            self.regions = sorted(set(self.region_of_code.values()))
            self.segments = sorted(set(self.segment_of_code.values()))
            self.seg_index = {name: i for i, name in enumerate(self.segments)}
            self.n_seg = len(self.segments)
            self.seg_adder = [int(adders.get(name.lower(), 0)) for name in 
self.segments]
    
        def _load_events(self):
            with open(self.path("events.json"), encoding="utf-8") as fh:
                events = json.load(fh)
            self.rows["events.json"] = len(events)
            self.events = {}
            for ev in events:
                self.events[ev["event_id"]] = {
                    "event_id": ev["event_id"],
                    "interval_count": int(ev["interval_count"]),
                    "cost_weight": int(ev["objective"]["cost_weight"]),
                    "recovery_weight": int(ev["objective"]["recovery_weight"]),
                    "start": 
datetime.datetime.fromisoformat(ev["dispatch_start"]),
                    "uplift": int(ev["uplift_millicents_per_kwh"]),
                }
            self.event_ids_sorted = sorted(self.events)
            self.season_order = sorted(
                self.events,
                key=lambda eid: (self.events[eid]["start"], desc_key(eid)),
            )
    
        def _load_sites(self):
            rows = read_rows(self.path("sites.csv"), ",")
            self.rows["sites.csv"] = len(rows)
            self.sites = {}
            self.unknown_region_site_rows = 0
            for r in rows:
                region = self.region_name(r["region_code"])
                if region is None:
                    self.unknown_region_site_rows += 1
                    continue
                seg_name = self.segment_name(r["segment_code"])
                self.sites[(r["event_id"], r["site_id"])] = {
                    "event_id": r["event_id"],
                    "site_id": r["site_id"],
                    "region": region,
                    "segment": seg_name,
                    "seg_idx": self.seg_index.get(seg_name, self.n_seg),
                    "beneficiary_id": r["beneficiary_id"],
                    "critical": r["critical"].strip() == "Y",
                }
    
        def _load_offers(self):
            rows = read_rows(self.path("offers.csv"), ",")
            self.rows["offers.csv"] = len(rows)
            self.offers = {}
            # every offer_id of offers.csv with the interval count of its event,
so
            # that profile rows of orphan offers are still classified by their 
kind
            self.offer_intervals = {}
            self.orphan_offer_rows = 0
            for r in rows:
                oid = r["offer_id"]
                event_id = r["event_id"]
                ic = self.events[event_id]["interval_count"]
                self.offer_intervals[oid] = ic
                key = (event_id, r["site_id"])
                if key not in self.sites:
                    self.orphan_offer_rows += 1
                    continue
                off = Offer(oid, event_id, r["site_id"], 
int(r["bid_cost_cents"]), ic)
                off.seg = self.sites[key]["seg_idx"]
                self.offers[oid] = off
    
        def _load_profiles(self):
            self.orphan_profile_rows = 0
            self.out_of_range_profile_rows = 0
            self.duplicate_profile_rows = 0
            wpt = self.wh_per_tick
            seen = set()
            count = 0
            with open(self.path("profiles.tsv"), newline="", encoding="utf-8") 
as fh:
                for r in csv.DictReader(fh, delimiter="\t"):
                    count += 1
                    oid = r["offer_id"]
                    ic = self.offer_intervals.get(oid)
                    if ic is None:
                        self.orphan_profile_rows += 1
                        continue
                    interval = int(r["interval"])
                    if interval < 1 or interval > ic:
                        self.out_of_range_profile_rows += 1
                        continue
                    key = (oid, interval)
                    if key in seen:
                        self.duplicate_profile_rows += 1
                        continue
                    seen.add(key)
                    off = self.offers.get(oid)
                    if off is None:
                        continue
                    mult = int(r["register_multiplier"])
                    off.rel[interval - 1] = to_ticks(int(r["relief_wh"]) * mult,
wpt)
                    off.rec[interval - 1] = to_ticks(int(r["recovery_wh"]) * 
mult, wpt)
            self.rows["profiles.tsv"] = count
            for off in self.offers.values():
                off.tot_rel = sum(off.rel)
                off.tot_rec = sum(off.rec)
    
        def _load_targets(self):
            rows = read_rows(self.path("targets.tsv"), "\t")
            self.rows["targets.tsv"] = len(rows)
            self.targets = {}
            wpt = self.wh_per_tick
            for r in rows:
                region = self.region_name(r["region_code"])
                if region is None:
                    continue
                self.targets[(r["event_id"], region, int(r["interval"]))] = (
                    to_ticks(int(r["target_wh"]), wpt),
                    int(r["shortfall_weight"]),
                )
    
        def _load_feeder_limits(self):
            self.ceilings = {}
            wpt = self.wh_per_tick
            count = 0
            for ln in fixed_width_lines(self.path("feeder_limits.txt")):
                count += 1
                event_id = ln[0:10].strip()
                region = self.region_name(ln[10:14])
                interval = int(ln[14:17])
                relief = int(ln[17:27])
                recovery = int(ln[27:37])
                if region is None:
                    continue
                self.ceilings[(event_id, region, interval)] = (
                    to_ticks(relief, wpt),
                    to_ticks(recovery, wpt),
                )
            self.rows["feeder_limits.txt"] = count
    
        def _load_site_state(self):
            self.state = {}
            count = 0
            with open(self.path("site_state.jsonl"), encoding="utf-8") as fh:
                for ln in fh:
                    if ln.strip() == "":
                        continue
                    count += 1
                    d = json.loads(ln)
                    key = (d["event_id"], d["site_id"])
                    if key not in self.sites:
                        continue
                    self.state[key] = (
                        bool(d["dispatch_authorized"]),
                        bool(d["safety_hold"]),
                        bool(d["cooldown"]),
                    )
            self.rows["site_state.jsonl"] = count
    
        def _load_waivers(self):
            rows = read_rows(self.path("continuity_waivers.csv"), ",")
            self.rows["continuity_waivers.csv"] = len(rows)
            self.waiver_signed_missing = 0
            self.waivers = {}
            for r in rows:
                signed = (r.get("waiver_signed") or "").strip()
                if signed == "":
                    self.waiver_signed_missing += 1
                key = (r["event_id"], r["site_id"])
                if key not in self.sites:
                    continue
                self.waivers[key] = (
                    r["routine_certificate_pending"].strip() == "Y",
                    r["waiver_status"].strip(),
                    signed,
                )
    
        def _load_floors(self):
            with open(self.path("segment_floors.json"), encoding="utf-8") as fh:
                floors = json.load(fh)["floors"]
            self.rows["segment_floors.json"] = len(floors)
            self.floors = {}
            wpt = self.wh_per_tick
            for f in floors:
                region = self.region_name(f["region_code"])
                seg = self.segment_name(f["segment_code"])
                if region is None or seg is None:
                    continue
                self.floors[(f["event_id"], region, seg)] = (
                    to_ticks(int(f["floor_wh"]), wpt),
                    int(f["priority_weight"]),
                )
    
        def _load_rate_cards(self):
            cards = 
ET.parse(self.path("rate_cards.xml")).getroot().findall("card")
            self.rows["rate_cards.xml"] = len(cards)
            self.cards = {}
            for c in cards:
                key = (c.get("event"), c.get("site"))
                if key not in self.sites:
                    continue
                base = int(c.find("base").text)
                override = c.find("override")
                self.cards[key] = (
                    base if override is None else int(override.text),
                    int(c.find("cap").text),
                )
    
        def _load_weights(self):
            rows = read_rows(self.path("recipient_weights.tsv"), "\t")
            self.rows["recipient_weights.tsv"] = len(rows)
            self.weights = {}
            for r in rows:
                key = (r["event_id"], r["site_id"])
                if key not in self.sites:
                    continue
                self.weights[key] = int(r["grant_weight"])
    
        def _load_pools(self):
            self.pools = {}
            count = 0
            for ln in fixed_width_lines(self.path("pools.txt")):
                count += 1
                event_id = ln[0:10].strip()
                region = self.region_name(ln[10:14])
                cents = int(ln[14:26])
                if region is None:
                    continue
                self.pools[(event_id, region)] = cents
            self.rows["pools.txt"] = count
    
        def _load_allowances(self):
            header, rows = read_commented_rows(self.path("site_allowances.tsv"))
            self.rows["site_allowances.tsv"] = len(rows)
            idx = {name: i for i, name in enumerate(header)}
            raw = {}
            for r in rows:
                draws = r[idx["draws_from"]].strip() if len(r) > 
idx["draws_from"] else ""
                raw[r[idx["site_id"]]] = (int(r[idx["allowance_ticks"]]), draws)
            self.allowance_root = {}
            self.allowance_ticks = {}
            for site in raw:
                cur = site
                seen = {cur}
                while raw[cur][1] and raw[cur][1] in raw:
                    cur = raw[cur][1]
                    if cur in seen:
                        break
                    seen.add(cur)
                self.allowance_root[site] = cur
                self.allowance_ticks[cur] = raw[cur][0]
    
        def _load_manifest(self):
            _, rows = read_commented_rows(self.path("export_manifest.tsv"))
            self.rows["export_manifest.tsv"] = len(rows)
    
        # ---------------- derived facts ----------------
    
        def _derive_labels(self):
            self.labels = {}
            for key, site in self.sites.items():
                authorized, hold, cooldown = self.state.get(key, (False, False, 
False))
                pending, status, signed = self.waivers.get(key, (False, "NONE", 
""))
                site["authorized"] = authorized
                waiver_valid = (
                    status == "VALID"
                    and signed == "Y"
                    and site["critical"]
                    and site["segment"] == "public"
                )
                if hold:
                    label = "SAFETY_HOLD"
                elif cooldown:
                    label = "COOLDOWN"
                elif pending and waiver_valid:
                    label = "CONTINUITY_WAIVED"
                elif pending:
                    label = "CERTIFICATE_PENDING"
                else:
                    label = "CLEAR"
                self.labels[key] = label
    
        def _derive_rates(self):
            for key, site in self.sites.items():
                start, cap = self.cards.get(key, (0, 0))
                adder = self.seg_adder[site["seg_idx"]] if site["seg_idx"] < 
self.n_seg else 0
                rate = start + adder + self.events[site["event_id"]]["uplift"]
                site["cap_binding"] = rate > cap
                site["rate"] = cap if rate > cap else rate
    
    
    def best_portfolio(ic, opts, target, weight, rceil, cceil, floor_terms, cw, 
rw, n_seg):
        """Enumerate feasible portfolios; return (selected offer ids, tie 
count).
    
        A portfolio holds at most one candidate offer per site. Adding an offer 
never
        lowers relief or recovery, so a branch that breaks a ceiling is pruned 
whole.
        """
        n = len(opts)
        relief = [0] * ic
        recov = [0] * ic
        segrel = [0] * (n_seg + 1)
        chosen = []
        rng = range(ic)
        best_key = None
        best_sel = ()
        tie_count = 0
    
        def walk(i, bid, rec_tot, nsel):
            nonlocal best_key, best_sel, tie_count
            if i == n:
                c1 = 0
                c4 = 0
                for t in rng:
                    d = target[t] - relief[t]
                    if d > 0:
                        c1 += weight[t] * d
                    elif d < 0:
                        c4 -= d
                c2 = 0
                for sidx, fticks, pw in floor_terms:
                    d = fticks - segrel[sidx]
                    if d > 0:
                        c2 += pw * d
                key = (c1, c2, cw * bid + rw * rec_tot, c4, nsel)
                if best_key is None or key < best_key:
                    best_key = key
                    best_sel = tuple(sorted(chosen))
                    tie_count = 1
                elif key == best_key:
                    tie_count += 1
                    cand = tuple(sorted(chosen))
                    if cand > best_sel:
                        best_sel = cand
                return
            walk(i + 1, bid, rec_tot, nsel)
            for off in opts[i]:
                orel = off.rel
                orec = off.rec
                ok = True
                for t in rng:
                    if relief[t] + orel[t] > rceil[t] or recov[t] + orec[t] > 
cceil[t]:
                        ok = False
                        break
                if not ok:
                    continue
                for t in rng:
                    relief[t] += orel[t]
                    recov[t] += orec[t]
                segrel[off.seg] += off.tot_rel
                chosen.append(off.oid)
                walk(i + 1, bid + off.bid, rec_tot + off.tot_rec, nsel + 1)
                chosen.pop()
                segrel[off.seg] -= off.tot_rel
                for t in rng:
                    relief[t] -= orel[t]
                    recov[t] -= orec[t]
    
        walk(0, 0, 0, 0)
        return best_sel, tie_count
    
    
    def largest_remainder(funded, entries):
        """Share `funded` cents over (beneficiary_id, weight) pairs with weight 
> 0.
    
        Returns (shares, cents handed out by remainder, residual tie flag).
        """
        total_w = sum(w for _, w in entries)
        shares = {}
        rems = []
        for bid, w in entries:
            q, r = divmod(funded * w, total_w)
            shares[bid] = q
            rems.append((r, bid))
        left = funded - sum(shares.values())
        rems.sort(key=lambda t: (t[0], t[1]), reverse=True)
        for i in range(left):
            shares[rems[i][1]] += 1
        nonzero = [r for r, _ in rems if r != 0]
        tie = len(nonzero) != len(set(nonzero))
        return shares, left, tie
    
    
    def allocate_grants(ex, funded, beneficiary_weights):
        """Returns (shares, allocated cents, residual cents, residual tie 
flag)."""
        positive = sorted((b, w) for b, w in beneficiary_weights.items() if w > 
0)
        if not positive:
            return {}, 0, 0, False
        claims = {b: w * ex.claim_cents_per_weight for b, w in positive}
        if sum(claims.values()) <= funded:
            return claims, sum(claims.values()), 0, False
        shares, residual, tie = largest_remainder(funded, positive)
        keep = [(b, w) for b, w in positive if shares[b] >= 
ex.minimum_grant_cents]
        if len(keep) == len(positive):
            return shares, funded, residual, tie
        if not keep:
            return {b: 0 for b, _ in positive}, 0, 0, False
        shares2, residual2, tie2 = largest_remainder(funded, keep)
        final = {b: 0 for b, _ in positive}
        final.update(shares2)
        return final, funded, residual2, tie2
    
    
    REGION_TOTAL_KEYS = (
        "candidate_offer_count",
        "selected_offer_count",
        "relief_ticks",
        "recovery_ticks",
        "shortfall_ticks",
        "overdelivery_ticks",
        "bid_cost_cents",
        "credit_cents",
        "grant_pool_cents",
        "grant_funded_cents",
        "grant_allocated_cents",
        "grant_unallocated_cents",
    )
    
    
    def derive(ex):
        """Run the season: candidates, portfolios, credits and grants per 
event-region."""
        regions = ex.regions
        n_seg = ex.n_seg
        denom = 1000 * ex.millicents_per_cent
        wpt = ex.wh_per_tick
    
        offers_by_er = {}
        for off in ex.offers.values():
            region = ex.sites[(off.event_id, off.site_id)]["region"]
            offers_by_er.setdefault((off.event_id, region), []).append(off)
        sites_by_er = {}
        for site in ex.sites.values():
            sites_by_er.setdefault((site["event_id"], site["region"]), 
[]).append(site)
    
        stats = {
            "ineligible_offer_count": 0,
            "allowance_blocked_offer_count": 0,
            "empty_candidate_groups": 0,
            "cap_binding_count": 0,
            "residual_cents": 0,
            "residual_tie_pool_count": 0,
            "unallocated_pool_count": 0,
        }
        tie_decisions = []
        allowance_used = dict.fromkeys(ex.allowance_ticks, 0)
        carried = dict.fromkeys(regions, 0)
        results = {}
    
        for event_id in ex.season_order:
            event = ex.events[event_id]
            ic = event["interval_count"]
            cw = event["cost_weight"]
            rw = event["recovery_weight"]
            relief_by_root = {}
    
            for region in regions:
                key_er = (event_id, region)
                site_rows = sites_by_er.get(key_er, [])
    
                candidates_by_site = {}
                candidate_count = 0
                for off in offers_by_er.get(key_er, []):
                    if not ex.sites[(off.event_id, off.site_id)]["authorized"]:
                        stats["ineligible_offer_count"] += 1
                        continue
                    root = ex.allowance_root.get(off.site_id, off.site_id)
                    remaining = ex.allowance_ticks.get(root, 0) - 
allowance_used.get(root, 0)
                    if off.tot_rel > remaining:
                        stats["allowance_blocked_offer_count"] += 1
                        continue
                    candidates_by_site.setdefault(off.site_id, []).append(off)
                    candidate_count += 1
                if candidate_count == 0:
                    stats["empty_candidate_groups"] += 1
    
                target = [0] * ic
                weight = [0] * ic
                rceil = [0] * ic
                cceil = [0] * ic
                for t in range(ic):
                    target[t], weight[t] = ex.targets.get((event_id, region, t +
1), (0, 0))
                    rceil[t], cceil[t] = ex.ceilings.get((event_id, region, t + 
1), (0, 0))
    
                floor_ticks_by_seg = [0] * n_seg
                floor_terms = []
                for i, seg_name in enumerate(ex.segments):
                    fticks, pw = ex.floors.get((event_id, region, seg_name), (0,
0))
                    floor_ticks_by_seg[i] = fticks
                    if fticks > 0 and pw > 0:
                        floor_terms.append((i, fticks, pw))
    
                opts = [candidates_by_site[s] for s in 
sorted(candidates_by_site)]
                selected_ids, tie_count = best_portfolio(
                    ic, opts, target, weight, rceil, cceil, floor_terms, cw, rw,
n_seg
                )
                if tie_count >= 2:
                    tie_decisions.append(
                        {
                            "event_id": event_id,
                            "region": region,
                            "tied_portfolio_count": tie_count,
                            "selected_offer_ids": list(selected_ids),
                        }
                    )
    
                relief = [0] * ic
                recov = [0] * ic
                bid_cost = 0
                credit_total = 0
                seg_stats = [
                    {"ids": [], "relief": 0, "recovery": 0, "bid": 0, "credit": 
0}
                    for _ in range(n_seg)
                ]
                for oid in selected_ids:
                    off = ex.offers[oid]
                    site = ex.sites[(off.event_id, off.site_id)]
                    rate = site["rate"]
                    credit = 0
                    for t in range(ic):
                        relief[t] += off.rel[t]
                        recov[t] += off.rec[t]
                        credited = off.rel[t] - off.rec[t]
                        if credited > 0:
                            credit += div_half_even(credited * wpt * rate, 
denom)
                    credit_total += credit
                    bid_cost += off.bid
                    if site["cap_binding"]:
                        stats["cap_binding_count"] += 1
                    if off.seg < n_seg:
                        st = seg_stats[off.seg]
                        st["ids"].append(oid)
                        st["relief"] += off.tot_rel
                        st["recovery"] += off.tot_rec
                        st["bid"] += off.bid
                        st["credit"] += credit
                    root = ex.allowance_root.get(off.site_id, off.site_id)
                    relief_by_root[root] = relief_by_root.get(root, 0) + 
off.tot_rel
    
                intervals = []
                shortfall_total = 0
                over_total = 0
                for t in range(ic):
                    gap = target[t] - relief[t]
                    short = gap if gap > 0 else 0
                    over = -gap if gap < 0 else 0
                    shortfall_total += short
                    over_total += over
                    intervals.append(
                        {
                            "interval": t + 1,
                            "target_ticks": target[t],
                            "relief_ticks": relief[t],
                            "recovery_ticks": recov[t],
                            "shortfall_ticks": short,
                            "overdelivery_ticks": over,
                        }
                    )
    
                pool = ex.pools.get(key_er, 0)
                funded = pool + carried[region] - credit_total
                if funded < 0:
                    funded = 0
    
                beneficiary_weights = {}
                recipients = 0
                for site in site_rows:
                    skey = (site["event_id"], site["site_id"])
                    if skey not in ex.weights:
                        continue
                    recipients += 1
                    w = ex.weights[skey]
                    if ex.labels[skey] == "CONTINUITY_WAIVED":
                        w *= 2
                    bid_key = site["beneficiary_id"]
                    beneficiary_weights[bid_key] = 
beneficiary_weights.get(bid_key, 0) + w
                if recipients == 0 or sum(beneficiary_weights.values()) == 0:
                    stats["unallocated_pool_count"] += 1
                    shares, allocated, residual, tie = {}, 0, 0, False
                else:
                    shares, allocated, residual, tie = allocate_grants(
                        ex, funded, beneficiary_weights
                    )
                stats["residual_cents"] += residual
                if tie:
                    stats["residual_tie_pool_count"] += 1
                unallocated = funded - allocated
                carried[region] = unallocated
    
                results[key_er] = {
                    "candidate_offer_count": candidate_count,
                    "selected_offer_ids": list(selected_ids),
                    "intervals": intervals,
                    "relief_total": sum(relief),
                    "recovery_total": sum(recov),
                    "shortfall_total": shortfall_total,
                    "over_total": over_total,
                    "bid_cost_cents": bid_cost,
                    "credit_cents": credit_total,
                    "grant_pool_cents": pool,
                    "grant_funded_cents": funded,
                    "grant_allocated_cents": allocated,
                    "grant_unallocated_cents": unallocated,
                    "grants": [
                        {"beneficiary_id": b, "cents": shares.get(b, 0)}
                        for b in sorted(b for b, w in 
beneficiary_weights.items() if w > 0)
                    ],
                    "compliance_counts": compliance_counts(ex, site_rows),
                    "seg_stats": seg_stats,
                    "floor_ticks": floor_ticks_by_seg,
                }
    
            for root, used in relief_by_root.items():
                allowance_used[root] = allowance_used.get(root, 0) + used
    
        return results, tie_decisions, stats
    
    
    def compliance_counts(ex, site_rows):
        counts = dict.fromkeys(LABELS, 0)
        for site in site_rows:
            counts[ex.labels[(site["event_id"], site["site_id"])]] += 1
        return counts
    
    
    def run(data_dir, out_dir):
        ex = Extract(data_dir)
        results, tie_decisions, stats = derive(ex)
    
        os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)
    
        def write(name, obj):
            with open(os.path.join(out_dir, name), "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
                json.dump(obj, fh, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))
                fh.write("\n")
    
        event_ids = ex.event_ids_sorted
        grand = dict.fromkeys(REGION_TOTAL_KEYS, 0)
        region_summary = {}
    
        for region in ex.regions:
            events_out = []
            totals = dict.fromkeys(REGION_TOTAL_KEYS, 0)
            for event_id in event_ids:
                res = results[(event_id, region)]
                events_out.append(
                    {
                        "event_id": event_id,
                        "candidate_offer_count": res["candidate_offer_count"],
                        "selected_offer_ids": res["selected_offer_ids"],
                        "intervals": res["intervals"],
                        "bid_cost_cents": res["bid_cost_cents"],
                        "credit_cents": res["credit_cents"],
                        "grant_pool_cents": res["grant_pool_cents"],
                        "grant_funded_cents": res["grant_funded_cents"],
                        "grant_allocated_cents": res["grant_allocated_cents"],
                        "grant_unallocated_cents": 
res["grant_unallocated_cents"],
                        "grants": res["grants"],
                        "compliance_counts": res["compliance_counts"],
                    }
                )
                totals["candidate_offer_count"] += res["candidate_offer_count"]
                totals["selected_offer_count"] += len(res["selected_offer_ids"])
                totals["relief_ticks"] += res["relief_total"]
                totals["recovery_ticks"] += res["recovery_total"]
                totals["shortfall_ticks"] += res["shortfall_total"]
                totals["overdelivery_ticks"] += res["over_total"]
                for key in (
                    "bid_cost_cents",
                    "credit_cents",
                    "grant_pool_cents",
                    "grant_funded_cents",
                    "grant_allocated_cents",
                    "grant_unallocated_cents",
                ):
                    totals[key] += res[key]
            write(
                "region_%s.json" % region,
                {
                    "region": region,
                    "events": events_out,
                    "totals": dict(totals, event_count=len(event_ids)),
                },
            )
            for key in REGION_TOTAL_KEYS:
                grand[key] += totals[key]
            region_summary[region] = {
                "selected_offer_count": totals["selected_offer_count"],
                "relief_ticks": totals["relief_ticks"],
                "bid_cost_cents": totals["bid_cost_cents"],
                "credit_cents": totals["credit_cents"],
                "grant_allocated_cents": totals["grant_allocated_cents"],
            }
    
        segment_summary = {}
        for i, seg_name in enumerate(ex.segments):
            entries = []
            totals = dict.fromkeys(
                (
                    "selected_offer_count",
                    "relief_ticks",
                    "recovery_ticks",
                    "bid_cost_cents",
                    "credit_cents",
                    "floor_ticks",
                ),
                0,
            )
            for event_id in event_ids:
                for region in ex.regions:
                    res = results[(event_id, region)]
                    st = res["seg_stats"][i]
                    floor_ticks = res["floor_ticks"][i]
                    relief = st["relief"]
                    if floor_ticks == 0:
                        bp = 10000
                    else:
                        bp = min(10000, div_half_up(10000 * relief, 
floor_ticks))
                    entries.append(
                        {
                            "event_id": event_id,
                            "region": region,
                            "selected_offer_ids": sorted(st["ids"]),
                            "relief_ticks": relief,
                            "recovery_ticks": st["recovery"],
                            "bid_cost_cents": st["bid"],
                            "credit_cents": st["credit"],
                            "floor_ticks": floor_ticks,
                            "floor_attainment_bp": bp,
                        }
                    )
                    totals["selected_offer_count"] += len(st["ids"])
                    totals["relief_ticks"] += relief
                    totals["recovery_ticks"] += st["recovery"]
                    totals["bid_cost_cents"] += st["bid"]
                    totals["credit_cents"] += st["credit"]
                    totals["floor_ticks"] += floor_ticks
            write(
                "segment_%s.json" % seg_name,
                {"segment": seg_name, "event_regions": entries, "totals": 
totals},
            )
            segment_summary[seg_name] = {
                "selected_offer_count": totals["selected_offer_count"],
                "relief_ticks": totals["relief_ticks"],
                "bid_cost_cents": totals["bid_cost_cents"],
                "credit_cents": totals["credit_cents"],
            }
    
        write(
            "summary.json",
            {
                "event_count": len(event_ids),
                "event_region_count": len(event_ids) * len(ex.regions),
                "site_count": len(ex.sites),
                "candidate_offer_count": grand["candidate_offer_count"],
                "selected_offer_count": grand["selected_offer_count"],
                "relief_ticks": grand["relief_ticks"],
                "recovery_ticks": grand["recovery_ticks"],
                "shortfall_ticks": grand["shortfall_ticks"],
                "overdelivery_ticks": grand["overdelivery_ticks"],
                "bid_cost_cents": grand["bid_cost_cents"],
                "credit_cents": grand["credit_cents"],
                "grant_pool_cents": grand["grant_pool_cents"],
                "grant_funded_cents": grand["grant_funded_cents"],
                "grant_allocated_cents": grand["grant_allocated_cents"],
                "grant_unallocated_cents": grand["grant_unallocated_cents"],
                "regions": region_summary,
                "segments": segment_summary,
            },
        )
    
        write(
            "policy_audit.json",
            {
                "compliance_counts": compliance_counts(ex, ex.sites.values()),
                "site_labels": [
                    {"event_id": e, "site_id": s, "label": ex.labels[(e, s)]}
                    for e, s in sorted(ex.sites)
                ],
                "tie_decisions": sorted(
                    tie_decisions, key=lambda d: (d["event_id"], d["region"])
                ),
                "cap_binding_count": stats["cap_binding_count"],
                "residual_cents": stats["residual_cents"],
                "residual_tie_pool_count": stats["residual_tie_pool_count"],
            },
        )
    
        zero_floor_count = 0
        for event_id in event_ids:
            for region in ex.regions:
                for seg_name in ex.segments:
                    if ex.floors.get((event_id, region, seg_name), (0, 0))[0] ==
0:
                        zero_floor_count += 1
    
        write(
            "data_quality.json",
            {
                "rows": ex.rows,
                "orphan_offer_rows": ex.orphan_offer_rows,
                "orphan_profile_rows": ex.orphan_profile_rows,
                "out_of_range_profile_rows": ex.out_of_range_profile_rows,
                "duplicate_profile_rows": ex.duplicate_profile_rows,
                "unknown_region_site_rows": ex.unknown_region_site_rows,
                "waiver_signed_missing": ex.waiver_signed_missing,
                "ineligible_offer_count": stats["ineligible_offer_count"],
                "allowance_blocked_offer_count": 
stats["allowance_blocked_offer_count"],
                "empty_candidate_groups": stats["empty_candidate_groups"],
                "zero_floor_count": zero_floor_count,
                "unallocated_pool_count": stats["unallocated_pool_count"],
            },
        )
    
    
    def main(argv):
        if len(argv) != 3:
            sys.stderr.write("usage: derive_report.py DATA_DIR OUT_DIR\n")
            return 2
        sys.setrecursionlimit(100000)
        run(argv[1], argv[2])
        return 0
    
    
    if __name__ == "__main__":
        sys.exit(main(sys.argv))

+26|def to_ticks(wh, wh_per_tick):
+27|    """A record's Wh value as ticks, rounded half up."""
+28|    return (2 * wh + wh_per_tick) // (2 * wh_per_tick)
+29|
+30|
+31|def div_half_up(num, den):
+32|    return (2 * num + den) // (2 * den)
+33|
+34|
+35|def div_half_even(num, den):
+36|    q, r = divmod(num, den)
+37|    twice = 2 * r
+38|    if twice > den or (twice == den and q % 2 == 1):
+39|        q += 1
+40|    return q
+41|
+42|
+43|def desc_key(text):
+44|    """Sort key that orders strings descending inside an ascending sort."""
+45|    return [-ord(c) for c in text]

+48|def read_rows(path, delimiter):
+49|    with open(path, newline="", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
+50|        return list(csv.DictReader(fh, delimiter=delimiter))

+53|def read_commented_rows(path):
+54|    """Header and data rows of a TSV file whose comment lines start with 
'#'."""
+55|    with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as fh:
+56|        lines = [ln.rstrip("\n").rstrip("\r") for ln in fh if not 
ln.startswith("#")]
+57|    lines = [ln for ln in lines if ln.strip() != ""]
+58|    if not lines:
+59|        return [], []
+60|    return lines[0].split("\t"), [ln.split("\t") for ln in lines[1:]]

+300|        self.waivers = {}
+301|        for r in rows:
+302|            signed = (r.get("waiver_signed") or "").strip()
+303|            if signed == "":
+304|                self.waiver_signed_missing += 1
+305|            key = (r["event_id"], r["site_id"])
+306|            if key not in self.sites:
+307|                continue
+308|            self.waivers[key] = (
+309|                r["routine_certificate_pending"].strip() == "Y",
+310|                r["waiver_status"].strip(),
+311|                signed,
+312|            )
+313|
+314|    def _load_floors(self):
+315|        with open(self.path("segment_floors.json"), encoding="utf-8") as 
fh:
+316|            floors = json.load(fh)["floors"]
+317|        self.rows["segment_floors.json"] = len(floors)
+318|        self.floors = {}
+319|        wpt = self.wh_per_tick
+320|        for f in floors:
+321|            region = self.region_name(f["region_code"])
+322|            seg = self.segment_name(f["segment_code"])
+323|            if region is None or seg is None:
+324|                continue
+325|            self.floors[(f["event_id"], region, seg)] = (
+326|                to_ticks(int(f["floor_wh"]), wpt),
+327|                int(f["priority_weight"]),
+328|            )
+329|
+330|    def _load_rate_cards(self):
+331|        cards = 
ET.parse(self.path("rate_cards.xml")).getroot().findall("card")
+332|        self.rows["rate_cards.xml"] = len(cards)
+333|        self.cards = {}
+334|        for c in cards:
+335|            key = (c.get("event"), c.get("site"))
+336|            if key not in self.sites:
+337|                continue
+338|            base = int(c.find("base").text)
+339|            override = c.find("override")
+340|            self.cards[key] = (
+341|                base if override is None else int(override.text),
+342|                int(c.find("cap").text),
+343|            )
+344|
+345|    def _load_weights(self):
+346|        rows = read_rows(self.path("recipient_weights.tsv"), "\t")
+347|        self.rows["recipient_weights.tsv"] = len(rows)
+348|        self.weights = {}
+349|        for r in rows:
+350|            key = (r["event_id"], r["site_id"])
+351|            if key not in self.sites:
+352|                continue
+353|            self.weights[key] = int(r["grant_weight"])
+354|
+355|    def _load_pools(self):
+356|        self.pools = {}
+357|        count = 0
+358|        for ln in fixed_width_lines(self.path("pools.txt")):
+359|            count += 1
+360|            event_id = ln[0:10].strip()
+361|            region = self.region_name(ln[10:14])
+362|            cents = int(ln[14:26])
+363|            if region is None:
+364|                continue
+365|            self.pools[(event_id, region)] = cents
+366|        self.rows["pools.txt"] = count
+367|
+368|    def _load_allowances(self):
+369|        header, rows = 
read_commented_rows(self.path("site_allowances.tsv"))
+370|        self.rows["site_allowances.tsv"] = len(rows)
+371|        idx = {name: i for i, name in enumerate(header)}
+372|        raw = {}
+373|        for r in rows:
+374|            draws = r[idx["draws_from"]].strip() if len(r) > 
idx["draws_from"] else ""
+375|            raw[r[idx["site_id"]]] = (int(r[idx["allowance_ticks"]]), 
draws)
+376|        self.allowance_root = {}
+377|        self.allowance_ticks = {}
+378|        for site in raw:
+379|            cur = site
+380|            seen = {cur}
+381|            while raw[cur][1] and raw[cur][1] in raw:
+382|                cur = raw[cur][1]
+383|                if cur in seen:
+384|                    break
+385|                seen.add(cur)
+386|            self.allowance_root[site] = cur
+387|            self.allowance_ticks[cur] = raw[cur][0]
+388|
+389|    def _load_manifest(self):
+390|        _, rows = read_commented_rows(self.path("export_manifest.tsv"))
+391|        self.rows["export_manifest.tsv"] = len(rows)
+392|
+393|    # ---------------- derived facts ----------------
+394|
+395|    def _derive_labels(self):
+396|        self.labels = {}
+397|        for key, site in self.sites.items():
+398|            authorized, hold, cooldown = self.state.get(key, (False, False,
False))
+399|            pending, status, signed = self.waivers.get(key, (False, "NONE",
""))
+400|            site["authorized"] = authorized
+401|            waiver_valid = (
+402|                status == "VALID"
+403|                and signed == "Y"
+404|                and site["critical"]
+405|                and site["segment"] == "public"
+406|            )
+407|            if hold:
+408|                label = "SAFETY_HOLD"
+409|            elif cooldown:
+410|                label = "COOLDOWN"
+411|            elif pending and waiver_valid:
+412|                label = "CONTINUITY_WAIVED"
+413|            elif pending:
+414|                label = "CERTIFICATE_PENDING"
+415|            else:
+416|                label = "CLEAR"
+417|            self.labels[key] = label
+418|
+419|    def _derive_rates(self):
+420|        for key, site in self.sites.items():
+421|            start, cap = self.cards.get(key, (0, 0))
+422|            adder = self.seg_adder[site["seg_idx"]] if site["seg_idx"] < 
self.n_seg else 0
+423|            rate = start + adder + self.events[site["event_id"]]["uplift"]
+424|            site["cap_binding"] = rate > cap
+425|            site["rate"] = cap if rate > cap else rate
+426|
+427|
+428|def best_portfolio(ic, opts, target, weight, rceil, cceil, floor_terms, cw,
rw, n_seg):
+429|    """Enumerate feasible portfolios; return (selected offer ids, tie 
count).
+430|
+431|    A portfolio holds at most one candidate offer per site. Adding an offer
never
+432|    lowers relief or recovery, so a branch that breaks a ceiling is pruned 
whole.
+433|    """
+434|    n = len(opts)
+435|    relief = [0] * ic
+436|    recov = [0] * ic
+437|    segrel = [0] * (n_seg + 1)
+438|    chosen = []
+439|    rng = range(ic)
+440|    best_key = None
+441|    best_sel = ()
+442|    tie_count = 0
+443|
+444|    def walk(i, bid, rec_tot, nsel):
+445|        nonlocal best_key, best_sel, tie_count
+446|        if i == n:
+447|            c1 = 0
+448|            c4 = 0
+449|            for t in rng:
+450|                d = target[t] - relief[t]
+451|                if d > 0:
+452|                    c1 += weight[t] * d
+453|                elif d < 0:
+454|                    c4 -= d
+455|            c2 = 0
+456|            for sidx, fticks, pw in floor_terms:
+457|                d = fticks - segrel[sidx]
+458|                if d > 0:
+459|                    c2 += pw * d
+460|            key = (c1, c2, cw * bid + rw * rec_tot, c4, nsel)
+461|            if best_key is None or key < best_key:
+462|                best_key = key
+463|                best_sel = tuple(sorted(chosen))
+464|                tie_count = 1
+465|            elif key == best_key:
+466|                tie_count += 1
+467|                cand = tuple(sorted(chosen))
+468|                if cand > best_sel:
+469|                    best_sel = cand
+470|            return
+471|        walk(i + 1, bid, rec_tot, nsel)
+472|        for off in opts[i]:
+473|            orel = off.rel
+474|            orec = off.rec
+475|            ok = True
+476|            for t in rng:
+477|                if relief[t] + orel[t] > rceil[t] or recov[t] + orec[t] > 
cceil[t]:
+478|                    ok = False
+479|                    break
+480|            if not ok:
+481|                continue
+482|            for t in rng:
+483|                relief[t] += orel[t]
+484|                recov[t] += orec[t]
+485|            segrel[off.seg] += off.tot_rel
+486|            chosen.append(off.oid)
+487|            walk(i + 1, bid + off.bid, rec_tot + off.tot_rec, nsel + 1)
+488|            chosen.pop()
+489|            segrel[off.seg] -= off.tot_rel
+490|            for t in rng:
+491|                relief[t] -= orel[t]
+492|                recov[t] -= orec[t]
+493|
+494|    walk(0, 0, 0, 0)
+495|    return best_sel, tie_count
+496|
+497|
+498|def largest_remainder(funded, entries):
+499|    """Share `funded` cents over (beneficiary_id, weight) pairs with weight
> 0.
+500|
+501|    Returns (shares, cents handed out by remainder, residual tie flag).
+502|    """
+503|    total_w = sum(w for _, w in entries)
+504|    shares = {}
+505|    rems = []
+506|    for bid, w in entries:
+507|        q, r = divmod(funded * w, total_w)
+508|        shares[bid] = q
+509|        rems.append((r, bid))
+510|    left = funded - sum(shares.values())
+511|    rems.sort(key=lambda t: (t[0], t[1]), reverse=True)
+512|    for i in range(left):
+513|        shares[rems[i][1]] += 1
+514|    nonzero = [r for r, _ in rems if r != 0]
+515|    tie = len(nonzero) != len(set(nonzero))
+516|    return shares, left, tie
+517|
+518|
+519|def allocate_grants(ex, funded, beneficiary_weights):
+520|    """Returns (shares, allocated cents, residual cents, residual tie 
flag)."""
+521|    positive = sorted((b, w) for b, w in beneficiary_weights.items() if w >
0)
+522|    if not positive:
+523|        return {}, 0, 0, False
+524|    claims = {b: w * ex.claim_cents_per_weight for b, w in positive}
+525|    if sum(claims.values()) <= funded:
+526|        return claims, sum(claims.values()), 0, False
+527|    shares, residual, tie = largest_remainder(funded, positive)
+528|    keep = [(b, w) for b, w in positive if shares[b] >= 
ex.minimum_grant_cents]
+529|    if len(keep) == len(positive):
+530|        return shares, funded, residual, tie
+531|    if not keep:
+532|        return {b: 0 for b, _ in positive}, 0, 0, False
+533|    shares2, residual2, tie2 = largest_remainder(funded, keep)
+534|    final = {b: 0 for b, _ in positive}
+535|    final.update(shares2)
+536|    return final, funded, residual2, tie2
+537|
+538|
+539|REGION_TOTAL_KEYS = (
+540|    "candidate_offer_count",
+541|    "selected_offer_count",
+542|    "relief_ticks",
+543|    "recovery_ticks",
+544|    "shortfall_ticks",
+545|    "overdelivery_ticks",
+546|    "bid_cost_cents",
+547|    "credit_cents",
+548|    "grant_pool_cents",
+549|    "grant_funded_cents",
+550|    "grant_allocated_cents",
+551|    "grant_unallocated_cents",
+552|)
+553|
+554|
+555|def derive(ex):
+556|    """Run the season: candidates, portfolios, credits and grants per 
event-region."""
+557|    regions = ex.regions
+558|    n_seg = ex.n_seg
+559|    denom = 1000 * ex.millicents_per_cent
+560|    wpt = ex.wh_per_tick
+561|
+562|    offers_by_er = {}
+563|    for off in ex.offers.values():
+564|        region = ex.sites[(off.event_id, off.site_id)]["region"]
+565|        offers_by_er.setdefault((off.event_id, region), []).append(off)
+566|    sites_by_er = {}
+567|    for site in ex.sites.values():
+568|        sites_by_er.setdefault((site["event_id"], site["region"]), 
[]).append(site)
+569|
+570|    stats = {
+571|        "ineligible_offer_count": 0,
+572|        "allowance_blocked_offer_count": 0,
+573|        "empty_candidate_groups": 0,
+574|        "cap_binding_count": 0,
+575|        "residual_cents": 0,
+576|        "residual_tie_pool_count": 0,
+577|        "unallocated_pool_count": 0,
+578|    }
+579|    tie_decisions = []
+580|    allowance_used = dict.fromkeys(ex.allowance_ticks, 0)
+581|    carried = dict.fromkeys(regions, 0)
+582|    results = {}
+583|
+584|    for event_id in ex.season_order:
+585|        event = ex.events[event_id]
+586|        ic = event["interval_count"]
+587|        cw = event["cost_weight"]
+588|        rw = event["recovery_weight"]
+589|        relief_by_root = {}
+590|
+591|        for region in regions:
+592|            key_er = (event_id, region)
+593|            site_rows = sites_by_er.get(key_er, [])
+594|
+595|            candidates_by_site = {}
+596|            candidate_count = 0
+597|            for off in offers_by_er.get(key_er, []):
+598|                if not ex.sites[(off.event_id, off.site_id)]["authorized"]:
+599|                    stats["ineligible_offer_count"] += 1
+600|                    continue
+601|                root = ex.allowance_root.get(off.site_id, off.site_id)
+602|                remaining = ex.allowance_ticks.get(root, 0) - 
allowance_used.get(root, 0)
+603|                if off.tot_rel > remaining:
+604|                    stats["allowance_blocked_offer_count"] += 1
+605|                    continue
+606|                candidates_by_site.setdefault(off.site_id, []).append(off)
+607|                candidate_count += 1
+608|            if candidate_count == 0:
+609|                stats["empty_candidate_groups"] += 1
+610|
+611|            target = [0] * ic
+612|            weight = [0] * ic
+613|            rceil = [0] * ic
+614|            cceil = [0] * ic
+615|            for t in range(ic):
+616|                target[t], weight[t] = ex.targets.get((event_id, region, t 
+ 1), (0, 0))
+617|                rceil[t], cceil[t] = ex.ceilings.get((event_id, region, t +
1), (0, 0))
+618|
+619|            floor_ticks_by_seg = [0] * n_seg
+620|            floor_terms = []
+621|            for i, seg_name in enumerate(ex.segments):
+622|                fticks, pw = ex.floors.get((event_id, region, seg_name), 
(0, 0))
+623|                floor_ticks_by_seg[i] = fticks
+624|                if fticks > 0 and pw > 0:
+625|                    floor_terms.append((i, fticks, pw))
+626|
+627|            opts = [candidates_by_site[s] for s in 
sorted(candidates_by_site)]
+628|            selected_ids, tie_count = best_portfolio(
+629|                ic, opts, target, weight, rceil, cceil, floor_terms, cw, 
rw, n_seg
+630|            )
+631|            if tie_count >= 2:
+632|                tie_decisions.append(
+633|                    {
+634|                        "event_id": event_id,
+635|                        "region": region,
+636|                        "tied_portfolio_count": tie_count,
+637|                        "selected_offer_ids": list(selected_ids),
+638|                    }
+639|                )
+640|
+641|            relief = [0] * ic
+642|            recov = [0] * ic
+643|            bid_cost = 0
+644|            credit_total = 0
+645|            seg_stats = [
+646|                {"ids": [], "relief": 0, "recovery": 0, "bid": 0, "credit":
0}
+647|                for _ in range(n_seg)
+648|            ]
+649|            for oid in selected_ids:
+650|                off = ex.offers[oid]
+651|                site = ex.sites[(off.event_id, off.site_id)]
+652|                rate = site["rate"]
+653|                credit = 0
+654|                for t in range(ic):
+655|                    relief[t] += off.rel[t]
+656|                    recov[t] += off.rec[t]
+657|                    credited = off.rel[t] - off.rec[t]
+658|                    if credited > 0:
+659|                        credit += div_half_even(credited * wpt * rate, 
denom)
+660|                credit_total += credit
+661|                bid_cost += off.bid
+662|                if site["cap_binding"]:
+663|                    stats["cap_binding_count"] += 1
+664|                if off.seg < n_seg:
+665|                    st = seg_stats[off.seg]
+666|                    st["ids"].append(oid)
+667|                    st["relief"] += off.tot_rel
+668|                    st["recovery"] += off.tot_rec
+669|                    st["bid"] += off.bid
+670|                    st["credit"] += credit
+671|                root = ex.allowance_root.get(off.site_id, off.site_id)
+672|                relief_by_root[root] = relief_by_root.get(root, 0) + 
off.tot_rel
+673|
+674|            intervals = []
+675|            shortfall_total = 0
+676|            over_total = 0
+677|            for t in range(ic):
+678|                gap = target[t] - relief[t]
+679|                short = gap if gap > 0 else 0
+680|                over = -gap if gap < 0 else 0
+681|                shortfall_total += short
+682|                over_total += over
+683|                intervals.append(
+684|                    {
+685|                        "interval": t + 1,
+686|                        "target_ticks": target[t],
+687|                        "relief_ticks": relief[t],
+688|                        "recovery_ticks": recov[t],
+689|                        "shortfall_ticks": short,
+690|                        "overdelivery_ticks": over,
+691|                    }
+692|                )
+693|
+694|            pool = ex.pools.get(key_er, 0)
+695|            funded = pool + carried[region] - credit_total
+696|            if funded < 0:
+697|                funded = 0
+698|
+699|            beneficiary_weights = {}
+700|            recipients = 0
+701|            for site in site_rows:
+702|                skey = (site["event_id"], site["site_id"])
+703|                if skey not in ex.weights:
+704|                    continue
+705|                recipients += 1
+706|                w = ex.weights[skey]
+707|                if ex.labels[skey] == "CONTINUITY_WAIVED":
+708|                    w *= 2
+709|                bid_key = site["beneficiary_id"]
+710|                beneficiary_weights[bid_key] = 
beneficiary_weights.get(bid_key, 0) + w
+711|            if recipients == 0 or sum(beneficiary_weights.values()) == 0:
+712|                stats["unallocated_pool_count"] += 1
+713|                shares, allocated, residual, tie = {}, 0, 0, False
+714|            else:
+715|                shares, allocated, residual, tie = allocate_grants(
+716|                    ex, funded, beneficiary_weights
+717|                )
+718|            stats["residual_cents"] += residual
+719|            if tie:
+720|                stats["residual_tie_pool_count"] += 1
+721|            unallocated = funded - allocated
+722|            carried[region] = unallocated
+723|
+724|            results[key_er] = {
+725|                "candidate_offer_count": candidate_count,
+726|                "selected_offer_ids": list(selected_ids),
+727|                "intervals": intervals,
+728|                "relief_total": sum(relief),
+729|                "recovery_total": sum(recov),
+730|                "shortfall_total": shortfall_total,
+731|                "over_total": over_total,
+732|                "bid_cost_cents": bid_cost,
+733|                "credit_cents": credit_total,
+734|                "grant_pool_cents": pool,
+735|                "grant_funded_cents": funded,
+736|                "grant_allocated_cents": allocated,
+737|                "grant_unallocated_cents": unallocated,
+738|                "grants": [
+739|                    {"beneficiary_id": b, "cents": shares.get(b, 0)}
+740|                    for b in sorted(b for b, w in 
beneficiary_weights.items() if w > 0)
+741|                ],
+742|                "compliance_counts": compliance_counts(ex, site_rows),
+743|                "seg_stats": seg_stats,
+744|                "floor_ticks": floor_ticks_by_seg,
+745|            }
+746|
+747|        for root, used in relief_by_root.items():
+748|            allowance_used[root] = allowance_used.get(root, 0) + used
+749|
+750|    return results, tie_decisions, stats
+751|
+752|
+753|def compliance_counts(ex, site_rows):
+754|    counts = dict.fromkeys(LABELS, 0)
+755|    for site in site_rows:
+756|        counts[ex.labels[(site["event_id"], site["site_id"])]] += 1
+757|    return counts
+758|
+759|
+760|def run(data_dir, out_dir):
+761|    ex = Extract(data_dir)
+762|    results, tie_decisions, stats = derive(ex)
+763|
+764|    os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)
+765|
+766|    def write(name, obj):
+767|        with open(os.path.join(out_dir, name), "w", encoding="utf-8") as 
fh:
+768|            json.dump(obj, fh, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))
+769|            fh.write("\n")
+770|
+771|    event_ids = ex.event_ids_sorted
+772|    grand = dict.fromkeys(REGION_TOTAL_KEYS, 0)
+773|    region_summary = {}
+774|
+775|    for region in ex.regions:
+776|        events_out = []
+777|        totals = dict.fromkeys(REGION_TOTAL_KEYS, 0)
+778|        for event_id in event_ids:
+779|            res = results[(event_id, region)]
+780|            events_out.append(
+781|                {
+782|                    "event_id": event_id,
+783|                    "candidate_offer_count": res["candidate_offer_count"],
+784|                    "selected_offer_ids": res["selected_offer_ids"],
+785|                    "intervals": res["intervals"],
+786|                    "bid_cost_cents": res["bid_cost_cents"],
+787|                    "credit_cents": res["credit_cents"],
+788|                    "grant_pool_cents": res["grant_pool_cents"],
+789|                    "grant_funded_cents": res["grant_funded_cents"],
+790|                    "grant_allocated_cents": res["grant_allocated_cents"],
+791|                    "grant_unallocated_cents": 
res["grant_unallocated_cents"],
+792|                    "grants": res["grants"],
+793|                    "compliance_counts": res["compliance_counts"],
+794|                }
+795|            )
+796|            totals["candidate_offer_count"] += res["candidate_offer_count"]
+797|            totals["selected_offer_count"] += 
len(res["selected_offer_ids"])
+798|            totals["relief_ticks"] += res["relief_total"]
+799|            totals["recovery_ticks"] += res["recovery_total"]
+800|            totals["shortfall_ticks"] += res["shortfall_total"]
+801|            totals["overdelivery_ticks"] += res["over_total"]
+802|            for key in (
+803|                "bid_cost_cents",
+804|                "credit_cents",
+805|                "grant_pool_cents",
+806|                "grant_funded_cents",
+807|                "grant_allocated_cents",
+808|                "grant_unallocated_cents",
+809|            ):
+810|                totals[key] += res[key]
+811|        write(
+812|            "region_%s.json" % region,
+813|            {
+814|                "region": region,
+815|                "events": events_out,
+816|                "totals": dict(totals, event_count=len(event_ids)),
+817|            },
+818|        )
+819|        for key in REGION_TOTAL_KEYS:
+820|            grand[key] += totals[key]
+821|        region_summary[region] = {
+822|            "selected_offer_count": totals["selected_offer_count"],
+823|            "relief_ticks": totals["relief_ticks"],
+824|            "bid_cost_cents": totals["bid_cost_cents"],
+825|            "credit_cents": totals["credit_cents"],
+826|            "grant_allocated_cents": totals["grant_allocated_cents"],
+827|        }
+828|
+829|    segment_summary = {}

recipients or zero weight means the whole pool is unallocated, claims totaling F

largest remainder.

Arguments:

  path: "/app/check.py"
  file_text: 
    """Independent brute-force cross-check of the portfolio choice and 
grants."""
    import csv, json, itertools, configparser, collections, sys, os
    sys.path.insert(0, '/app/out')
    import derive_report as dr
    
    D = '/app/data'
    ex = dr.Extract(D)
    results, ties, stats = dr.derive(ex)
    
    # ---- brute force a sample of event-regions with itertools.product ----
    # replicate candidate sets by replaying the season (needed for allowances)
    offers_by_er = collections.defaultdict(list)
    for off in ex.offers.values():
        offers_by_er[(off.event_id, ex.sites[(off.event_id, 
off.site_id)]['region'])].append(off)
    
    used = collections.defaultdict(int)
    bf_mismatch = 0
    bf_checked = 0
    tie_total = 0
    for event_id in ex.season_order:
        evt = ex.events[event_id]
        ic = evt['interval_count']; cw = evt['cost_weight']; rw = 
evt['recovery_weight']
        per_root = collections.defaultdict(int)
        for region in ex.regions:
            key = (event_id, region)
            cand = collections.defaultdict(list)
            for off in offers_by_er[key]:
                if not ex.sites[(off.event_id, off.site_id)]['authorized']:
                    continue
                root = ex.allowance_root[off.site_id]
                if off.tot_rel > ex.allowance_ticks[root] - used[root]:
                    continue
                cand[off.site_id].append(off)
            tgt = [0]*ic; sw = [0]*ic; rc = [0]*ic; cc = [0]*ic
            for t in range(ic):
                tgt[t], sw[t] = ex.targets.get((event_id, region, t+1), (0, 0))
                rc[t], cc[t] = ex.ceilings.get((event_id, region, t+1), (0, 0))
            fl = []
            for i, seg in enumerate(ex.segments):
                f, pw = ex.floors.get((event_id, region, seg), (0, 0))
                fl.append((f, pw))
            groups = [[None]+v for v in (cand[s] for s in sorted(cand))]
            best = None; bestsel = None; cnt = 0
            for combo in itertools.product(*groups):
                sel = [o for o in combo if o is not None]
                rel = [0]*ic; rec = [0]*ic
                for o in sel:
                    for t in range(ic):
                        rel[t] += o.rel[t]; rec[t] += o.rec[t]
                if any(rel[t] > rc[t] or rec[t] > cc[t] for t in range(ic)):
                    continue
                c1 = sum(sw[t]*max(0, tgt[t]-rel[t]) for t in range(ic))
                segrel = [0]*(ex.n_seg+1)
                for o in sel:
                    segrel[o.seg] += o.tot_rel
                c2 = sum(fl[i][1]*max(0, fl[i][0]-segrel[i]) for i in 
range(ex.n_seg))
                c3 = cw*sum(o.bid for o in sel) + rw*sum(rec)
                c4 = sum(max(0, rel[t]-tgt[t]) for t in range(ic))
                k = (c1, c2, c3, c4, len(sel))
                ids = tuple(sorted(o.oid for o in sel))
                if best is None or k < best:
                    best = k; bestsel = ids; cnt = 1
                elif k == best:
                    cnt += 1
                    if ids > bestsel:
                        bestsel = ids
            bf_checked += 1
            if cnt >= 2:
                tie_total += 1
            got = results[key]['selected_offer_ids']
            if list(bestsel) != got:
                bf_mismatch += 1
                if bf_mismatch < 5:
                    print('MISMATCH', key, bestsel, got)
            for o in (ex.offers[i] for i in got):
                per_root[ex.allowance_root[o.site_id]] += o.tot_rel
        for r, v in per_root.items():
            used[r] += v
    
    print('brute-forced event-regions', bf_checked, 'mismatches', bf_mismatch)
    print('brute-force tie groups', tie_total, 'program tie groups', len(ties))
    
    # ---- cross checks of the written outputs ----
    OUT = '/app/out'
    load = lambda n: json.load(open(os.path.join(OUT, n)))
    summ = load('summary.json')
    regs = {r: load('region_%s.json' % r) for r in ex.regions}
    segs = {s: load('segment_%s.json' % s) for s in ex.segments}
    pa = load('policy_audit.json')
    
    for k in ('relief_ticks', 'recovery_ticks', 'bid_cost_cents', 
'credit_cents',
              'shortfall_ticks', 'overdelivery_ticks', 'grant_pool_cents',
              'grant_funded_cents', 'grant_allocated_cents', 
'grant_unallocated_cents',
              'selected_offer_count', 'candidate_offer_count'):
        tot = sum(regs[r]['totals'][k] for r in ex.regions)
        assert tot == summ[k], (k, tot, summ[k])
    for k in ('relief_ticks', 'recovery_ticks', 'bid_cost_cents', 
'credit_cents',
              'selected_offer_count'):
        tot = sum(segs[s]['totals'][k] for s in ex.segments)
        assert tot == summ[k], ('seg', k, tot, summ[k])
    print('region/segment totals agree with summary')
    
    # region per-event internal consistency
    for r, doc in regs.items():
        assert doc['region'] == r
        assert [e['event_id'] for e in doc['events']] == sorted(ex.events)
        acc = collections.Counter()
        for e in doc['events']:
            assert [i['interval'] for i in e['intervals']] == list(
                range(1, ex.events[e['event_id']]['interval_count']+1))
            for i in e['intervals']:
                assert i['shortfall_ticks'] == max(0, 
i['target_ticks']-i['relief_ticks'])
                assert i['overdelivery_ticks'] == max(0, 
i['relief_ticks']-i['target_ticks'])
                acc['relief_ticks'] += i['relief_ticks']
                acc['recovery_ticks'] += i['recovery_ticks']
                acc['shortfall_ticks'] += i['shortfall_ticks']
                acc['overdelivery_ticks'] += i['overdelivery_ticks']
            assert e['selected_offer_ids'] == sorted(e['selected_offer_ids'])
            assert sum(e['compliance_counts'].values()) == 5
            assert e['grant_allocated_cents'] + e['grant_unallocated_cents'] == 
e['grant_funded_cents']
            assert sum(g['cents'] for g in e['grants']) == 
e['grant_allocated_cents']
            assert [g['beneficiary_id'] for g in e['grants']] == sorted(
                g['beneficiary_id'] for g in e['grants'])
        for k, v in acc.items():
            assert doc['totals'][k] == v, (r, k, v, doc['totals'][k])
    print('region files internally consistent')
    
    for s, doc in segs.items():
        order = [(e['event_id'], e['region']) for e in doc['event_regions']]
        assert order == sorted(order) and len(order) == 2000
        for e in doc['event_regions']:
            if e['floor_ticks'] == 0:
                assert e['floor_attainment_bp'] == 10000
            else:
                exp = min(10000, dr.div_half_up(10000*e['relief_ticks'], 
e['floor_ticks']))
                assert e['floor_attainment_bp'] == exp
    print('segment files internally consistent')
    
    # all chosen offers appear in exactly one region file and one segment file
    reg_sel = collections.Counter()
    for r, doc in regs.items():
        for e in doc['events']:
            reg_sel.update(e['selected_offer_ids'])
    seg_sel = collections.Counter()
    for s, doc in segs.items():
        for e in doc['event_regions']:
            seg_sel.update(e['selected_offer_ids'])
    assert all(v == 1 for v in reg_sel.values())
    assert set(seg_sel) == set(reg_sel), (len(seg_sel), len(reg_sel))
    print('selected offers consistent across files:', len(reg_sel))
    
    # no floats / booleans anywhere
    def scan(o, path=''):
        if isinstance(o, bool):
            raise AssertionError('bool at '+path)
        if isinstance(o, float):
            raise AssertionError('float at '+path)
        if isinstance(o, dict):
            for k, v in o.items():
                scan(v, path+'/'+str(k))
        elif isinstance(o, list):
            for i, v in enumerate(o):
                scan(v, path+'/%d' % i)
    scan([load(n) for n in os.listdir(OUT) if n.endswith('.json')])
    print('no floats or booleans in any output')
    
    # labels independently recomputed
    sites = list(csv.DictReader(open(D+'/sites.csv')))
    state = {}
    for ln in open(D+'/site_state.jsonl'):
        d = json.loads(ln); state[(d['event_id'], d['site_id'])] = d
    wv = {}
    for r in csv.DictReader(open(D+'/continuity_waivers.csv')):
        wv[(r['event_id'], r['site_id'])] = r
    exp = {}
    for r in sites:
        k = (r['event_id'], r['site_id'])
        st = state[k]; w = wv[k]
        valid = (w['waiver_status'] == 'VALID' and w['waiver_signed'] == 'Y'
                 and r['critical'] == 'Y' and r['segment_code'] == 'P')
        if st['safety_hold']:
            lab = 'SAFETY_HOLD'
        elif st['cooldown']:
            lab = 'COOLDOWN'
        elif w['routine_certificate_pending'] == 'Y' and valid:
            lab = 'CONTINUITY_WAIVED'
        elif w['routine_certificate_pending'] == 'Y':
            lab = 'CERTIFICATE_PENDING'
        else:
            lab = 'CLEAR'
        exp[k] = lab
    got = {(d['event_id'], d['site_id']): d['label'] for d in pa['site_labels']}
    assert got == exp, 'label mismatch'
    assert pa['compliance_counts'] == dict(collections.Counter(exp.values())) | 
{
        k: pa['compliance_counts'][k] for k in pa['compliance_counts']
        if k not in collections.Counter(exp.values())}
    print('site labels match an independent recomputation')
    print('OK')
    

  kind: "FileEditorAction"

Agent Action ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Summary: Create synthetic extract test with expectations

Reasoning:
I confirm that an independent brute-force enumeration (no pruning) produces 
identical selections with zero ties, so ties genuinely don't occur in this data.
The brute force runs in under 2 seconds because candidate counts per 
event-region are small, but zero ties worries me since the grader checks 
tie_decisions against fresh runs.

I should build a synthetic test extract to actually exercise the tie logic, 
largest-remainder branch, minimum grant and second-share rules, unknown-region 
rows, orphan/duplicate profiles, carry chains, overrides, caps, and 
allowance-blocking — all untested paths in my implementation. Designing a small 
extract with 2 events, 2 regions, 2 segments, and a few sites would let me 
hand-compute expected outputs to verify correctness.

I'm sketching the program config and events file now, defining units, 
region/segment codes, segment rate adders, and grant parameters, then will write
offers deliberately set up to trigger a tie and a largest-remainder scenario 
with minimum grant thresholds.

Now I'm planning two events, E2 and E1, with different dispatch start times in 
different timezones that both normalize to similar UTC windows, deliberately 
testing whether season ordering versus event_id sorting is handled correctly. 
I'm also starting to lay out specific sites under each event with carefully 
chosen cost/recovery weights so I can hand-verify the expected output.

Given how quickly the manual complexity grows—multiple sites with different 
benchmarks, authorization statuses, and unknown region codes—I'm deciding it's 
more reliable to build a synthetic extract generator and verify results against 
an independently reimplemented brute-force check script, rather than computing 
every grant by hand. My plan is to construct a small extract specifically 
designed to exercise each branch, then run the program against it.

I'm concretely designing the test parameters now: wh_per_tick, 
millicents_per_cent, region mappings, segment adders, grant claim rates and 
minimums, then starting to define the actual events.

EV-A starts exactly at 00:00Z while EV-B starts at 01:00+01:00, which is the 
same instant — a deliberate tie to test the descending-id tiebreaker rule, so 
EV-B should be reached first. I'm giving EV-B distinct ic, cost_weight, 
recovery_weight, and uplift values, then beginning to lay out the sites.csv rows
mapping each event to its regions, segments, benchmark IDs, and flags.

I'm also adding an unknown-region row for EV-A and reusing site SA1 across both 
events to test cross-event site handling, plus setting up site_state fields

CONTINUITY_WAIVED, CERTIFICATE_PENDING, or a SAFETY_HOLD flag that need separate
tallying - counting out to 7 CLEAR, 1 COOLDOWN, 1 CONTINUITY_WAIVED, 2 
CERTIFICATE_PENDING, and the remaining SAFETY_HOLD case.

I catch a miscount and recheck row 14, confirming the total hits 13 as expected.
Then I move on to listing the site allowance IDs and tallying recipient weight 
values across the EV-A pairings.

Now I'm looking at the pools file, which only has 6 entries covering 
EV-B/EV-A/EV-C against R1/R2/B1/A1 combos, and I notice Green entries entirely 
and several EV-B/EV-C pairings are missing — likely intentional for testing 
defaults or fallback behavior.

Total pool sums to 2118 across all entries. Working through season order EV-B, 
EV-A, EV-C, I'm tracing each event-region allocation: EV-B/Amber and EV-B/Blue 
both have zero or near-zero available funds after credits, so nothing gets 
distributed; EV-B/Red has 998 remaining after a credit, and I'm starting to 
allocate that to the SA1 recipient with weight 3.

The claim of 120 for BEN1 gets paid in full since it's under the 998 limit, 
leaving 878 to carry forward into EV-A/Red. In EV-A, Amber allocates its full 7 
units with no leftover, Blue distributes 100 units using shares 45/46/9 with a 
tie on the residual, Green has nothing to allocate, and Red combines its credit 
with the carried 878 to reach 885 available, against claims totaling 160 across 
three beneficiaries that get paid in full.

That 160 payout leaves 725 unallocated to carry into EV-C's Red bucket. EV-C's 
Amber, Blue, and Green all have zero funding, while Red accumulates 1725 from 
the base plus carry, pays out 200 to BEN1, and leaves 1525 unallocated. Totaling
everything up, funded reaches 3715 against 587 allocated, leaving 3128 
unallocated overall, distributed as 878 in EV-B/Red, 725 in EV-A/Red, and 1525 
in EV-C/Red.

Now I'm tallying residual cents at 1, counting groups with no recipients or zero
weight (7 total across the three events), and identifying empty candidate groups
per event — 3 for EV-A, 2 for EV-B, and 4 for EV-C.

Checking the zero-floor count: out of 24 combinations across events, regions, 
and segments, only two are non-zero, leaving 22. I'm also noting the 
event-region count at 12, site count at 13, offer counts (6 candidates, 3 
selected), and tick values for relief, recovery, and overdelivery before working
through the per-interval targets.

For EV-A with intercept 2, I'm tabulating values across Amber, Blue, Green, and 
Red regions for both intervals, tracking which show zero versus which have a 
baseline that decays to a smaller value with an associated weight. For EV-B with
intercept 1, I'm doing the same breakdown, finding most regions at zero except 
Blue.

Now I'm tallying up the shortfalls and overdeliveries: EV-A's Blue and Green 
regions are short by 50 and 60 respectively, EV-C's Red is short by 100 for a 
total shortfall of 210, while EV-B's Blue overdelivers by 50. I'm now working 
out feeder ceiling values for relief and recovery, assigning generous limits to 
regions that need them and zero to ones that don't.

Next I'm defining floor entries — EV-A/Red/P at 1500 wh, EV-B/Blue/P at 500 wh, 
and EV-C/Red/C at zero — three total, each with their priority weights. Then I 
move to setting up rate cards, one per site row including SX1, before circling 
back to verify the EV-A/Red optimum against its floor.

Checking the three-way tie for EV-A/Red again: with floor 150 ticks and pw 0, c2
comes out to 0, confirming OFF-A4 as the correct pick since it has the greatest 
id among the tied offers OFF-A1, OFF-A3, and OFF-A4. I also want to make sure 
the tie count reported is 3, matching the expected tie_decisions structure, 
before moving on to building out the region_Red.json output.

Now I'm tallying totals for region_Red across events EV-A, EV-B, EV-C: summing 
candidate offer counts to 5, selected offers to 2, relief to 200, recovery to 
70, shortfall to 100, bid amounts to 800, credit to 5, pool to 2010, funded to 
3608, allocated to 480, and unallocated to 3128, then starting the same

getting overall summary figures: relief 300, recovery 70, shortfall 210, over 
50, bid 1200, credit 7, pool 2118, funded 3715, allocated 587, unallocated 3128.
I'm now cross-checking that the pool total across all four regions adds up 
correctly.

Checking the segment breakdown confirms three offers selected across commercial 
and public segments with matching relief, bid, and credit totals, and now I'm

Recovery figures add up to 70, matching expectations. Now I'm tallying row 
counts across all the data quality files to make sure each output file has the
